# 网页导航

Reviewed topic membership; resources can appear in multiple topics.

Full source records: https://awesomecua.com/downloads/topics/web-navigation/records.jsonl

## 论文与综述

- [Securing Web Agents with the Compile-Then-Execute Paradigm](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2611-06039/) — 2026-11-04
  Baya compiles a web task into a program using a Trusted Website API before viewing live content, limiting how webpage prompt injections can alter agent control flow.
- [JHiMA-WebRL: A Joint Hierarchical Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Framework for Long-Horizon Web Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2611-05263/) — 2026-11-03
  Introduces JHiMA-WebRL, which jointly trains a task decomposer and GUI actor with hierarchical RL for long-horizon web tasks in WebArena.
- [Voluntary Servitude: Weaponized Safety Warning Mechanisms for Controlling AI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2611-04393/) — 2026-11-03
  Studies fabricated hazard warnings in reviews that steer agents’ choices without explicit commands, including transfer to browser agents, and tests a corroboration requirement.
- [WebHarbor: Docking Real Websites for Evolving GUI Agent Environments](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2611-03634/) — 2026-11-02
  Introduces WebHarbor, a suite of 153 executable website environments and 3,518 tasks for evaluating and training GUI web agents on interactive workflows.
- [From Local Skills to Long-Horizon Web Tasks: Progressive Skill Exploration for LLM Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2611-03116/) — 2026-11-02
  Introduces SCOPE, a training method for long web-agent tasks that discovers local skills from rollouts, composes them into chains, and expands them using failure experience.
- [When 200 OK Is Not an Answer: Silent Fetch Failures in Agentic Web Content Acquisition](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2611-00884/) — 2026-10-31
  Studies silent failures in web content fetched by static and rendered-browser clients, and evaluates checks for whether HTTP 200 pages contain usable content.
- [PatchScout: Thematic Web Data Collection via Information Foraging](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2611-00169/) — 2026-10-30
  Introduces PatchScout, a multi-agent method for collecting thematic documents from the live web by deciding when to explore a new information region.
- [ANGLER: Verifier-Grounded Benchmarking with an Adaptive Attacker for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-41272/) — 2026-10-30
  Introduces REEF, a website benchmark with state-based attack verification, and ANGLER, which adapts attack lures using outcomes from web-agent rollouts.
- [AgentShield: Fortifying Web Applications Against AI Agents and Agentic Browsers](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-41181/) — 2026-10-30
  Evaluates agentic browsers against web anti-bot defenses and introduces AgentShield, a behavioral fingerprinting framework for distinguishing agents from people.
- [DynaRev: Benchmarking Web Agents under Dynamic Goal Revision](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-40133/) — 2026-10-29
  Introduces DynaRev, a benchmark of 181 web-agent tasks whose goals change during execution, with trajectory-based measures of correctness, responsiveness, and efficiency.
- [InterA11y: Interactive Web Accessibility Error Detection with LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-39488/) — 2026-10-29
  Introduces InterA11y, a web accessibility evaluation system that guides LLM agents with rubrics and tools for inspecting and operating pages.
- [AgentHeal: Evaluating Goal-Preserving Resource Recovery of Web Agents under Access Barriers](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-39234/) — 2026-10-29
  Introduces AgentHeal, a benchmark of whether web agents detect access barriers, find legitimate alternative sources, and preserve the user's original goal.
- [WebAnything: From the Wild Web to Scalable Agent Environments](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-37786/) — 2026-10-27
  Introduces WebAnything, a framework that reconstructs reusable, resettable website environments from real URLs for web-agent training and evaluation.
- [Right Click, Wrong Box: Grading GUI Grounding by What a Click Does](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-36831/) — 2026-10-27
  Proposes grading GUI grounding clicks by their executed outcomes and audits box-based scores on live web applications and ScreenSpot-v2 screenshots.
- [Back to Basics: A Skill-Centric Approach to Generalist Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-35823/) — 2026-10-26
  Introduces MiniWeb for skill-labeled browser tasks, MiniWebGen for generating training trajectories, and MiniWebAgent for composing learned skills into web workflows.
- [Harness-Model Collaboration for Long-Horizon Continual Learning](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-34724/) — 2026-10-26
  Introduces HaMo, a continual-learning framework for web agents that retrieves strategy cards from past workflows and updates model parameters to reuse them.
- [AgentProofBench: Benchmarking Reusable Verifiers for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-34634/) — 2026-10-25
  Introduces AgentProofBench, a benchmark linking tests of reusable web-agent verification rules to subsequent execution from saved web states.
- [SkillShift-Bench: Benchmarking Skill Learning under Environment Evolution for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-33528/) — 2026-10-24
  Introduces SkillShift-Bench to evaluate how web agents reuse learned skills when task context, visual perception, or execution conditions change across web systems.
- [In-Context Teacher–Student Guidance for Open-Weight Browser Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-a5dc446fab5e6bdce517/) — 2026-10-19
  Training-free protocol that turns a teacher model's offline reasoning traces into prompt guidance for open-weight browser agents; on MiniWoB++ it lifts solved student-task pairs from 208 to 271 across ten students.
- [Deep Search in Real-World Web Environments](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-30181/) — 2026-10-19
  OpenDS-World benchmarks multi-step research on the live open Web, requiring agents to search, interact with browsers and combine evidence across sources.
- [SPIDES: Information-Flow Control for Browser Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-24277/) — 2026-10-16
  SPIDES propagates HTML integrity and confidentiality labels into agent context and mediates browser actions with information-flow policies and approval gates, evaluated in the labeled MicroNet ecosystem.
- [PersonaCUABench: Benchmarking Computer-Use Agents Serving Diverse Personas](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-22990/) — 2026-10-16
  PersonaCUABench varies simulated user requests and clarification dialogue over fixed web tasks, comparing task completion with requirement handling and persona-agent satisfaction ratings.
- [What It Is, Not What It Says: Securing Agentic Browsing Interactions with Web Semantics](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-22697/) — 2026-10-15
  Intact records webpage element provenance and capabilities, isolates planning per element, and uses a browser harness to constrain actions and request consent for third-party interactions.
- [Don't Retrain, Remix: Adaptation of Web Agents using Mixture Hypernetworks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-22539/) — 2026-10-15
  WebMix uses a scout-produced interface manual to route mixtures of source-trained LoRA adapters for web agents, adapting to target interfaces without target-time gradient updates.
- [Why Generate a Click? Plug-in Decision Heads for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-20107/) — 2026-10-14
  Adds decision heads to a frozen vision-language generator for selecting web actions and targets, with a confidence gate that falls back to text generation.
- [Teaching Agents in their Own Style: Blackbox Teacher Distillation for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-19834/) — 2026-10-14
  Introduces WebKD, which repairs failed student web-agent trajectories with minimal teacher edits and trains on corrections that preserve the student's reasoning style.
- [Reading Right, Acting Wrong: Diagnosing Decision Failures of System One Models in Agent Loops](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-18579/) — 2026-10-13
  Pairs factual probes with next-action choices on frozen agent traces, including Mind2Web, to diagnose reading-decision gaps without closed-loop execution.
- [BrowseSafe++: Benchmarking Prompt Injection Detection Across Web Contexts, Languages, and Browser Views](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-17457/) — 2026-10-12
  Extends BrowseSafe with paired attacked and clean webpages in several representations, and studies text and screenshot detectors plus feedback-adapted prompt injections.
- [Glasseek: A Transparent Recipe for Training and Trustworthy Evaluation of DeepSearch Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-16998/) — 2026-10-12
  Introduces Glasseek, a framework for deep-search agents that explore the open web, with trajectory construction, training across context compression, and a test-set leakage audit.
- [HyWe: Unifying Search and Operation for Real-World Hybrid Web Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-16319/) — 2026-10-12
  Introduces HyWe, which switches between information search and browser operation within one trajectory, alongside a demonstration pipeline and a benchmark of hybrid web tasks.
- [A Baseline Assessment for the Agentic Web Technology Stack](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-13703/) — 2026-10-09
  Presents MASTIF, a framework for comparing combinations of agent models, protocols, and implementation frameworks on web tasks, with an initial assessment using 300 Mind2Web tasks.
- [Web$hell: A Unified Interface for Agentic Web Access](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-13416/) — 2026-10-09
  Introduces Web$hell, an interface that compiles live pages into persistent information and action objects so agents can gather evidence, navigate websites, and reuse acquired content through shared operations.
- [Can LLMs Fix It Without Code? Toward Automated Verification of No-Code Bug Fixes](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-11963/) — 2026-10-08
  Tests natural-language no-code fixes through GUI execution in Linux Brave. Scripted fixed/buggy-state gates and issue-specific checkers distinguish fix outcomes from the executor's completion claims.
- [Secure-CUA: Controlling Untrusted Influence in Computer-Use Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-09469/) — 2026-10-07
  Proposes Secure-CUA, which commits to per-action transactions before reading untrusted page content and separates content queries from GUI target selection. The paper formalizes its protection assumptions and evaluates benign task utility on WebArena.
- [CLIFT: Conformal Self-Verification for Web Agent Training and Test-Time Scaling](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-06829/) — 2026-10-05
  Introduces CLIFT, which certifies agent-generated verification signals against training-time judges and reuses them for per-step rewards and trajectory selection. At inference, a frozen question bank helps compare candidate web rollouts without querying an external judge.
- [HyperBrowseComp: A Multilingual and Multimodal Stress Test for Web-Browsing Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-03574/) — 2026-10-02
  Introduces HyperBrowseComp, a multilingual benchmark for browsing agents that must connect evidence across open-web sources, including videos, scans, images, and maps.
- [WebFovea: When the Model Is Right but the Click Is Wrong -- Reliable Round Trips for Vision-Based Web Agents on Live Websites](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-03036/) — 2026-10-02
  Describes WebFovea, a vision-based live-web agent whose execution loop checks action parsing, browser effects, feedback accuracy, and observation delivery. The report analyzes failures such as coordinate mismatches and silent control interactions and documents corresponding harness changes.
- [WebUIProof: Benchmarking WebUI Code Generators with UI-Agent Execution Harness](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-02617/) — 2026-10-02
  Introduces an execution-based benchmark for generated web interfaces, with a headless-browser UI-agent harness that checks DOM interactions and functional assertions.
- [AgentBreaker: Evaluating Context-Aware Indirect Prompt Injection Risks in Modern Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-e86b3a1454b494224f07/) — 2026-10-01
  AgentBreaker generates page-specific prompt injections in HTML elements to test web agents, then evaluates attacks and defenses on 60 webpages from Online-Mind2Web.
- [How To Train Your World Model: Fine-tuning vs RAG for LM-based World Modeling](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-02542/) — 2026-10-01
  Compares fine-tuned, retrieval-based and hybrid language-model world models for next-observation prediction and action selection in text environments including WebShop.
- [Action Conditioned Bisimulation For GUI Agent Memory](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-38778/) — 2026-09-30
  Defines a GUI-memory merge rule that treats two states as equivalent only when shared actions lead to matching outcomes and successor states. The training-free method replaces observation-similarity merging and is tested in closed-loop MiniWoB++ interaction.
- [AdaptArena: Evaluating Test-Time Personalization of Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-36488/) — 2026-09-29
  Introduces AdaptArena to test whether web agents can infer unstated user preferences from relevant past trajectories and carry those preferences into new tasks. Its AdaptiveAgent baseline separates preference-retrieval challenges from failures in downstream web execution.
- [Structured Interaction, Visual Localization, and Robust Execution for Complex Web Tasks: A Technical Report on the WebRetriever Challenge](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-35904/) — 2026-09-28
  Describes a WebRetriever agent that combines semantic webpage operations with visual localization when structured representations are insufficient. Hierarchical context management and fault-aware execution support processing tasks across concurrent browsers.
- [Probe to Act: Elevating Browser-Use Agent via Active Visual Probing](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-33646/) — 2026-09-27
  Introduces Probe to Act, which lets browser agents request visual evidence linking DOM handles and screen regions before committing an operation. It retains selected interaction evidence as memory and studies both prompted use and distillation into open models.
- [ORBIT: A Framework for Multi-Agent Safety and Security Evaluations](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-33102/) — 2026-09-27
  Inspect-based framework that varies multi-agent roles, communication, attacks and defenses, with BrowserGym and virtual-desktop GUI adapters alongside coding and other tool scenarios.
- [Learning from Others, Acting for You: Cross-User Memory Sharing for LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-32511/) — 2026-09-26
  ShareMem consolidates reusable experience across users while keeping preference retrieval user-bound; its web evaluation predicts target elements and actions on recorded Mind2Web states.
- [From Trajectories to Grounded Preferences: Process Preference Synthesis via Interaction Element Graphs for Web PRMs](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-32351/) — 2026-09-26
  Introduces SURFPRM, which turns web demonstrations into an interaction-element graph for synthesizing grounded negative actions. Its contrastive preference data trains process reward models to distinguish plausible on-page alternatives and guide downstream trajectory search.
- [The Hard Part Comes After Search: Benchmarking Web Agents on Synthesizing, Organizing, and Displaying Knowledge](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-30604/) — 2026-09-24
  KNOWS benchmarks browser workflows that retrieve and synthesize information into documents, presentations and spreadsheets. Task-specific evaluators combine deterministic checks with model judgments, distinguishing partial progress from usable end-to-end artifact completion.
- [EconSkills: Studying Skill Transfer and Retrieval for Web Agents on Live Economic Data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-19523/) — 2026-09-17
  EconSkills converts verified economic-data browsing traces into parameterized procedures with navigation, verification and recovery guidance. Its evaluation separates known-skill transfer from library retrieval, exposing the limits of approximate matches to new tasks.
- [The Verifiable Action Card: Trustworthy Human-in-the-Loop Control for Secure Autonomous Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-18411/) — 2026-09-16
  Presents the Verifiable Action Card (VAC), a browser approval mechanism that derives confirmations from pending actions and binds user approval to execution.
- [RiskChainBench: A Benchmark for Obfuscated Platform Message Restoration and Evidence-Grounded Web Investigation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-16900/) — 2026-09-15
  Introduces RiskChainBench, pairing obfuscated-message restoration with web-agent investigation of linked sites in labeled local web environments.
- [AutoTailor: Automatic, User-Aligned Capability Selection and Adaptation for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-13548/) — 2026-09-11
  AutoTailor compiles web trajectories into reusable browser-automation APIs, then filters and dynamically revises the capability inventory. WebArena experiments distinguish API-only operation from an agent that can fall back to ordinary reasoning and browser actions.
- [Token Efficient Task Execution via Application Behavior Modeling for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-13491/) — 2026-09-11
  OdoBot builds a model of web-application behavior from successful task demonstrations and uses it to guide task execution. Its Canvas LMS evaluation compares token use and completion with existing web agents.
- [When and What to Teach: Budget-Aware Online Adaptation for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-05513/) — 2026-08-31
  Proposes an online teaching framework that decides which web-agent episodes warrant a stronger teacher and which trajectory turns merit training. A solvability gate and score-guided trimming aim to preserve first-attempt performance while reducing teaching and training costs.
- [SCAFFOLD: Self-Improving Web Agents via Recursive Parametric Skill Abstraction](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-05511/) — 2026-08-31
  Introduces SCAFFOLD, which extracts executable skills from web trajectories, composes them into a hierarchy, and compresses redundant skills through behavioral checks. It periodically distills skill-guided trajectories into the policy and studies repeated improvement across web benchmarks.
- [Learning Simple Test-Time Environments for LLM Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2608-29305/) — 2026-08-29
  Introduces Test-Time Environment Decomposition, which uses trial interaction to break complex web observations into simpler modules. A label-free adaptation procedure learns from these sub-environments and studies whether their gains compose in full web tasks.
- [BrowserForge: Scaling Web Episode via Parallel Browser Sandboxes](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2608-24848/) — 2026-08-25
  Introduces BrowserForge, which combines broad website sourcing, parallel browser sandboxes, and task-proposer/solver interaction to collect verified web trajectories. The pipeline uses page structure during synthesis but trains a policy that acts from screenshots.
- [Wuying-Browser-Agent: Real-World Centric Fundamental Long-Horizon Browser Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2608-17319/) — 2026-08-18
  Combines a browser execution framework, recovery-oriented supervision, and online reinforcement learning, with a bilingual live-web task benchmark.
- [Improving LLM-based Autonomous Web Agents with Filtering](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2609-27770/) — 2026-08-16
  Trains HTML-element relevance filters on Mind2Web trajectories and transfers them to WebArena to reduce irrelevant browser context. It also evaluates a zero-shot ColBERT retriever and documents baseline web-agent failure modes.
- [SynWeaver: Website-Prior Task and Trajectory Co-Synthesis for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2608-12429/) — 2026-08-12
  SynWeaver explores a website to build a map of page states and executable interactions, then learns website priors for task proposals. Joint task/trajectory synthesis revises inconsistent pairs and verifies or repairs the resulting supervision.
- [SkillLens: Visual Skill Cards for Retrieval-Augmented GUI Action Prediction and On-Policy Distillation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2608-10775/) — 2026-08-11
  Introduces SkillLens visual skill cards for retrieved GUI action guidance and CardDistill for training an action-prediction student that runs without retrieval on website tasks.
- [CAP: A Scalable Benchmark for Evaluating Cross-Site Browser Agents with Complex Actions and Perception](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2608-08392/) — 2026-08-09
  CAP builds cross-site browser workflows by decomposing websites into operation and perception requirements and recomposing them into tasks. Its judge-based evaluation diagnoses complex interactions and visual-perception failures across real websites.
- [SkillTracer: Structural Failure Attribution and Refinement of Agentic Skills in Long-Horizon Web Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-a2c0216a0d8875e86dee/) — 2026-08-08
  SkillTracer maps web agent skills to inspectable plan graphs, uses interaction traces to locate failures, and refines reusable skills across long tasks and episodes.
- [WebRider: Persona-Conditioned Intent Controllers for Live-Web Assistance](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2608-06704/) — 2026-08-07
  Introduces WebRider, a live-web agent that maintains intent contracts while carrying out guarded browser actions, and RiderBench for auditing those contracts across websites.
- [StepJack: Benchmarking Computer-Use Agent Safety Against Multi-Step Indirect Prompt Injection](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2608-06477/) — 2026-08-06
  Introduces StepJack, a benchmark of indirect prompt injections that split an adversarial goal across pages on a computer-use agent's web navigation path.
- [From Monoliths to Swarms: A Study of Attack Surface Evolution in the Transition to Multi-Agent Web Systems](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2608-00202/) — 2026-07-31
  Categorizes structural attack surfaces that emerge when single-agent browser systems are decomposed into multi-agent web workflows.
- [From Blind Edits to Verified Repair: Building Trustworthy User-Side LLM Agents for Web Accessibility](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2608-24913/) — 2026-07-26
  Builds a local browser-extension agent that generates and verifies additive CSS repairs for WCAG web accessibility violations.
- [Broken Gates: Re-evaluating Web Bot Defenses in the Age of LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2607-18659/) — 2026-07-21
  Measures how autonomous LLM browser agents interact with commercial bot-management and anti-automation defenses on the web.
- [Designing Agent-Ready Websites for AI Web Agents: A Framework for Machine Readability, Actionability, and Decision Reliability](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2607-12056/) — 2026-07-13
  Proposes an agent-ready website design framework for e-commerce browser agents and compares it with a human-oriented site on shopping tasks, outcomes, steps, and token use.
- [Prismata: Confining Cross-Site Prompt Injection in Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2607-08147/) — 2026-07-09
  Introduces Prismata, a defense for web agents that assigns trust labels to page content and enforces them by redacting content and restricting agent actions.
- [WebRetriever: A Large-Scale Comprehensive Benchmark for Efficient Web Agent Evaluation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2607-06118/) — 2026-07-07
  WebRetriever evaluates navigation, knowledge-assisted interaction and end-to-end information-extraction workflows under separate protocols. Its NavEval judge uses richer interaction context to diagnose cases where navigation success and useful task completion diverge.
- [Untrusted Content Masking for Web Agents with Security Guarantees](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2607-05277/) — 2026-07-06
  Untrusted Content Masking uses DOM structure to redact untrusted webpage regions before agent observation. A sandboxed interaction interface separates privileges so browser actions can proceed under the proposed trust-boundary model without exposing protected content to the planner.
- [Navigation Alone Is Not Enough: Evaluating Explanatory Assistive UI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2608-09944/) — 2026-07-01
  Introduces NeXUI, an assistive UI-agent benchmark that evaluates navigation together with interface-grounded explanations and user oversight in recorded interface states.
- [Ko-WideSearch: A Korean Breadth-Search Benchmark for Exhaustive Set Enumeration by Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-27595/) — 2026-06-25
  Introduces Ko-WideSearch, a Korean web-agent benchmark that asks agents to find every member of a set and fill a table of attributes for each member.
- [Empowering GUI Agents via Autonomous Experience Exploration and Hindsight Experience Utilization for Task Planning](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-27330/) — 2026-06-25
  PEEU explores web environments and turns hindsight experience into aligned high-level planning supervision. A hierarchical task-decomposition analysis examines why atomic-skill competence alone falls short of planning generalization across websites.
- [Fara-1.5: Scalable Learning Environments for Computer Use Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-20785/) — 2026-06-18
  Builds browser-agent training trajectories with live and synthetic environments, solvers, user simulation, and complementary correctness and efficiency verifiers.
- [When Web Agents Finish but Still Fail: Reproducible Triggers and Trace Diagnostics for Parallel Web Exploration](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-20724/) — 2026-06-16
  Introduces Parallel WebBench for parallel web exploration and uses verified URL trajectories to diagnose search loops, incomplete answers, and failures after evidence retrieval.
- [Beyond Domains: Reusing Web Skills via Transferable Interaction Patterns](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-17645/) — 2026-06-16
  Introduces SkillMigrator, a web agent that turns successful interactions into reusable skills and transfers them between sites by matching page layouts and live element references.
- [Running the Gauntlet: Hard Agentic Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-14397/) — 2026-06-12
  Introduces GauntletBench, a web-based benchmark of vision-intensive tasks in five professional applications for assessing agent perception, 3D reasoning, and generalisation.
- [Who Pays the Price? Stakeholder-Centric Prompt Injection Benchmarking for Real-world Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-13385/) — 2026-06-11
  StakeBench evaluates prompt-injection harm to different stakeholders in shopping web-agent workflows. Its executable cases separate attack objectives and assess both outcomes and execution processes to reveal how the same attack pattern can affect different parties.
- [MemVenom: Triggered Poisoning of Multimodal Memories in Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-10742/) — 2026-06-09
  Introduces MemVenom, a black-box attack that plants coordinated text and image content in web-agent memory to trigger retrieval and redirect later actions.
- [WebChallenger: A Reliable and Efficient Generalist Web Agent](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-10423/) — 2026-06-09
  WebChallenger builds a hierarchical DOM-derived page representation for selective observation, reusable site maps and compound actions. The framework combines these mechanisms without model fine-tuning and evaluates generalization across multiple web-agent benchmarks.
- [AliyunConsoleAgent: Training Web Agents in Real-World Cloud Environments via Distillation and Reinforcement Learning](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-09447/) — 2026-06-08
  AliyunConsoleAgent trains browser agents to verify documented procedures against changing cloud-console UIs through trajectory distillation and online GRPO. Provisioned rollout environments and backend audit-log checks support training and outcome evaluation.
- [RunAgent SuperBrowser: A Theory of Autonomous Web Navigation Grounded in Human Browsing Behaviour](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-09399/) — 2026-06-08
  SUPERBROWSER combines screenshot-based target proposals with separate orchestration, planning and action roles. A bounded ledger and context-eviction process retain recent actions, task facts and checkpoints during browser execution.
- [AsyncWebRL: Efficient Asynchronous Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Step Visual Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-05597/) — 2026-06-04
  AsyncWebRL overlaps web-agent rollouts, updates and policy refresh while managing screenshot handling and a persistent rollout pool. It also replaces trajectory-length normalization in multi-step GRPO to reduce verbose failed trajectories, with throughput and out-of-distribution evaluation.
- [Online Skill Learning for Web Agents via State-Grounded Dynamic Retrieval](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-04391/) — 2026-06-03
  State-Grounded Dynamic Retrieval extracts reusable sub-procedures from completed web trajectories and represents them as paired text and code. Skills are retrieved against both the current webpage state and the task goal at intermediate execution steps.
- [OpenWebRL: Demystifying Online Multi-turn Reinforcement Learning for Visual Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-02031/) — 2026-06-01
  Introduces OpenWebRL, a framework for training visual web agents with online multi-turn reinforcement learning on live websites, including browser infrastructure and success judging.
- [PersonaTrail: Benchmarking Personalized Web Agents through Browsing Trails](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2607-20482/) — 2026-05-30
  Introduces PersonaTrail, a benchmark for personalized web agents using browsing histories, and PACMem, a structured memory method for guiding navigation.
- [Masking Stale Observations Helps Search Agents -- Until It Doesn't: A Regime Map and Its Mechanism](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-00408/) — 2026-05-29
  Studies when masking older retrieved pages helps long-horizon search agents manage context, using offline and live-web search benchmarks and analysis of reopened pages.
- [Learning to Adapt: Self-Improving Web Agent via Cognitive-Aware Exploration](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-31365/) — 2026-05-29
  Introduces SCALE, which uses exploration roles and graph planning to train web agents, and SCALE-20k, a dataset of tasks and demonstrations from real websites.
- [Revisiting Observation Reduction for Web Agents: Comprehensive Evaluation with a Lightweight Framework](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-29397/) — 2026-05-28
  Introduces a lightweight framework for evaluating HTML reduction in web agents, using Minimal Failure Set coverage to assess pruning and optimize latency.
- [GTA: Generating Long-Horizon Tasks for Web Agents at Scale](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-29218/) — 2026-05-28
  GTA generates multi-hop web-agent tasks and executable trajectories through crawling, retrieval, and validation, releasing a benchmark built from over 50 websites.
- [PRO-CUA: Process-Reward Optimization for Computer Use Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-29119/) — 2026-05-27
  PRO-CUA trains computer-use agents with step-level RL, scoring candidate actions from live rollouts with a process reward model and evaluating on live web benchmarks.
- [PANDO: Efficient Multimodal AI Agents via Online Skill Distillation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-24785/) — 2026-05-24
  Analyzes inefficiency in VisualWebArena trajectories and distills reusable online skills to reduce repeated actions and token overhead in multimodal web agents.
- [Agent JIT Compilation for Latency-Optimizing Web Agent Planning and Scheduling](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-21470/) — 2026-05-20
  Agent JIT compilation turns browser task descriptions into validated executable plans containing model calls, tools and parallel work. A cost-based scheduler and pre/postcondition protocol target latency and incorrect tool use in web-agent execution.
- [Weasel: Out-of-Domain Generalization for Web Agents via Importance-Diversity Data Selection](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-20291/) — 2026-05-19
  Weasel selects web-agent training steps by balancing importance and diversity across states, sites and interaction patterns. It also prunes training accessibility trees around known action targets and regenerates rationales to match the model’s reasoning style.
- [DocOS: Towards Proactive Document-Guided Actions in GUI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-18048/) — 2026-05-18
  DocOS benchmarks agents that search online documentation and translate procedural instructions into GUI actions in interactive web environments. It separates failures to retrieve useful procedures from failures to ground retrieved instructions into executable actions.
- [Skim: Speculative Execution for Fast and Efficient Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-16565/) — 2026-05-15
  Introduces Skim, a speculative execution framework for web agents that learns site URL and answer patterns offline, then uses them to answer matching queries with a small model.
- [ShopGym: An Integrated Framework for Realistic Simulation and Scalable Benchmarking of E-Commerce Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-16116/) — 2026-05-15
  ShopGym builds sandbox e-commerce storefronts from live shops and generates catalog-grounded tasks for evaluating web shopping agents in resettable environments.
- [PersonaFingerprint: Measuring Persona Inference on Modern Websites with LLM-Driven Browsing](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-15962/) — 2026-05-15
  PersonaFingerprint studies persona inference from encrypted web traffic, using a persona-constrained computer-use agent to browse real websites and collect traces.
- [SaaS-Bench: Can Computer-Use Agents Leverage Real-World SaaS to Solve Professional Workflows?](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-15777/) — 2026-05-15
  SaaS-Bench evaluates browser agents on professional workflows across self-hosted applications. Weighted checkpoints measure task completion and partial progress, with application-state checks and configured content judges documented in the official harness.
- [Known By Their Actions: Fingerprinting LLM Browser Agents via UI Traces](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-14786/) — 2026-05-14
  A study shows that websites can infer the model behind a browser agent from passively recorded UI actions and timing traces across web tasks.
- [SimPersona: Learning Discrete Buyer Personas from Raw Clickstreams for Grounded E-Commerce Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-14205/) — 2026-05-14
  SimPersona learns buyer types from clickstreams and uses persona tokens to condition web agents trained on browsing traces for live storefront tasks.
- [WebTrap: Stealthy Mid-Task Hijacking of Browser Agents During Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-08310/) — 2026-05-08
  WebTrap injects mid-task instructions into browser-agent navigation to steer an agent toward an attacker goal before it resumes the user's task.
- [Region4Web: Rethinking Observation Space Granularity for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-07134/) — 2026-05-08
  Reorganizes webpage accessibility trees into functional regions through hierarchical decomposition and semantic abstraction. PageDigest supplies a persistent region-level observation to web agents, evaluated on WebArena.
- [WAAA! Web Adversaries Against Agentic Browsers](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-05509/) — 2026-05-06
  Evaluates agentic browsers against classical web attacks and visual social-engineering lures beyond indirect prompt injection.
- [cotomi Act: Learning to Automate Work by Watching You](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-03231/) — 2026-05-04
  cotomi Act is a browser agent that executes multi-step tasks and turns observed user browsing into persistent knowledge shared with users.
- [BaRA: Budget-constrained and Reliable Web Data Collection Agent](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2607-00007/) — 2026-05-02
  Introduces BaRA, a web data-collection agent that discovers live site links, checks extracted text, images, and videos, and uses history to recover from failures.
- [AutoSurfer -- Teaching Web Agents through Comprehensive Surfing, Learning, and Modeling](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-27253/) — 2026-04-29
  AutoSurfer explores website actions breadth-first, retaining page and trajectory knowledge to guide task synthesis and trajectory refinement. It evaluates the resulting training data by fine-tuning a web agent on WebArena and examining synthesized-task diversity.
- [DRIVE: Modeling Skills at the Reasoning and Interaction Levels for Web Agents under Continual Learning](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2605-23939/) — 2026-04-28
  DRIVE separates reusable reasoning skills from page-specific interaction skills for web agents, retrieving both to plan tasks and execute actions on websites.
- [Benchmarking Web Agent Safety under E-commerce Deceptive Interfaces](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-13686/) — 2026-04-26
  Introduces WebDecept, a plugin for injecting deceptive e-commerce interface patterns during web-agent tasks and evaluating how multimodal agents respond.
- [PageGuide: Browser extension to assist users in navigating a webpage and locating information](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-23772/) — 2026-04-26
  PageGuide is a browser extension that highlights evidence for answers on a page and guides users through web pages with evidence captured at each step.
- [Mango: Multi-Agent Web Navigation via Global-View Optimization](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-18779/) — 2026-04-20
  Proposes Mango, a multi-agent web-navigation method that selects starting URLs using Thompson Sampling and stores navigation history to inform later attempts.
- [WebUncertainty: Dual-Level Uncertainty Driven Planning and Reasoning For Autonomous Web Agent](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-17821/) — 2026-04-20
  WebUncertainty adapts planning mode to task uncertainty and guides action-level tree search with confidence-derived uncertainty estimates. Its evaluations examine autonomous execution of dynamic, long-horizon web tasks on WebArena and WebVoyager.
- [RiskWebWorld: A Realistic Interactive Benchmark for GUI Agents in E-commerce Risk Management](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-13531/) — 2026-04-15
  Introduces RiskWebWorld, an interactive benchmark of e-commerce risk-management tasks for GUI agents on websites, with Gymnasium infrastructure for evaluation and agentic RL.
- [WebXSkill: Skill Learning for Autonomous Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-13318/) — 2026-04-14
  WebXSkill pairs parameterized action programs with step-level language guidance, mining reusable skills from synthetic browser-agent traces. A URL-based graph supports retrieval, and deployment alternates between direct program execution and guided native planning.
- [From Imitation to Discrimination: Progressive Curriculum Learning for Robust Web Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-12666/) — 2026-04-14
  Introduces the Triton dataset and a staged training curriculum for text-based web navigation agents, using hard-negative HTML examples to train element discrimination and longer task execution.
- [WebForge: Breaking the Realism-Reproducibility-Scalability Trilemma in Browser Agent Benchmark](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-10988/) — 2026-04-13
  Presents WebForge, a pipeline that plans, generates, refines and validates self-contained interactive websites. WebForge-Bench uses these environments to evaluate browser agents across domains and controlled difficulty levels.
- [ClawBench: Can AI Agents Complete Everyday Online Tasks?](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-08523/) — 2026-04-09
  Introduces ClawBench, 153 everyday tasks on production websites in 15 categories; an interception layer blocks each final submission so bookings, purchases and job applications are scored without side effects.
- [MolmoWeb: Open Visual Web Agent and Open Data for the Open Web](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-08516/) — 2026-04-09
  Presents MolmoWebMix, combining synthetic browser trajectories, human demonstrations and GUI perception data, and MolmoWeb screenshot-to-action policies trained from it. Browser benchmarks also test parallel rollouts with best-of-N selection.
- [Structured Distillation of Web Agent Capabilities Enables Generalization](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-07776/) — 2026-04-09
  Introduces Agent-as-Annotators, which uses modular LLM roles to synthesize and filter web-agent trajectories for supervised training of a smaller web agent.
- [Where Did It Go Wrong? Process-Level Evaluation of Web Agents with Semantic State Tracking](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2606-15673/) — 2026-04-08
  Introduces WebStep, a web-agent benchmark that records semantic states behind GUI interactions to analyze exploration, execution accuracy, and decisive errors across task steps.
- [WebSP-Eval: Evaluating Web Agents on Website Security and Privacy Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-06367/) — 2026-04-07
  WebSP-Eval evaluates agents carrying out website security and privacy tasks such as preference changes and session management. A browser extension controls account and initial state across runs, enabling analysis of failures on stateful UI controls.
- [The Tool Illusion: Rethinking Tool Use in Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-03465/) — 2026-04-03
  Presents a controlled study of higher-level tool use in web agents across tool sources, backbone models, frameworks, and evaluation benchmarks.
- [Poison Once, Exploit Forever: Environment-Injected Memory Poisoning Attacks on Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-02623/) — 2026-04-03
  Studies persistent web-agent memory contamination introduced through environmental observations. Experiments on WebArena and VisualWebArena examine cross-session effects and how interaction failures alter susceptibility.
- [Read More, Think More: Revisiting Observation Reduction for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-01535/) — 2026-04-02
  Studies HTML and accessibility-tree observations for web agents, relating their usefulness to model capability and thinking budget, and proposes diff-based observation history to save tokens.
- [When Users Change Their Mind: Evaluating Interruptible Agents in Long-Horizon Web Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-00892/) — 2026-04-01
  Introduces InterruptBench, a WebArena-Lite-derived benchmark that tests how long-horizon web-navigation agents handle mid-task user additions, revisions, and retractions.
- [Emergence WebVoyager: Toward Consistent and Transparent Evaluation of (Web) Agents in The Wild](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2603-29020/) — 2026-03-30
  Audits sources of ambiguity and operational variation in WebVoyager evaluation and introduces standardized guidance for task setup, failure handling, annotation and reporting. Emergence WebVoyager applies this protocol to a web-agent evaluation.
- [StressWeb: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Web Agent Robustness under Realistic Interaction Variability](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2604-16385/) — 2026-03-27
  Introduces a web-agent stress-testing benchmark with controlled changes to layouts, interaction semantics, and execution for diagnosing behavior under varied conditions.
- [WebTestBench: Evaluating Computer-Use Agents towards End-to-End Automated Web Testing](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2603-25226/) — 2026-03-26
  Introduces WebTestBench for end-to-end website testing, separating checklist generation from defect detection. Its WebTester baseline evaluates test coverage, detection and sustained interaction across web application categories.
- [The Cognitive Firewall:Securing Browser Based AI Agents Against Indirect Prompt Injection Via Hybrid Edge Cloud Defense](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2603-23791/) — 2026-03-24
  Proposes a split-compute browser defense pairing a local visual sentinel, a cloud planner, and deterministic policy enforcement against indirect prompt injection.
- [IntentWeave: A Progressive Entry Ladder for Multi-Surface Browser Agents in Cloud Portals](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2603-22917/) — 2026-03-24
  Introduces IntentWeave, a design space and browser-extension prototype for placing web-agent assistance across browser surfaces, evaluated through a study of three entry strategies.
- [Ego2Web: A Web Agent Benchmark Grounded in Egocentric Videos](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2603-22529/) — 2026-03-23
  Ego2Web pairs first-person videos with online tasks that require agents to connect the user’s physical context to web actions. It also evaluates an automatic task judge against human judgments and analyzes failures in video understanding and web execution.
- [ContractSkill: Repairable Contract-Based Skills for Multimodal Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2603-20340/) — 2026-03-20
  Turns draft web-agent skills into structured executable artifacts that support deterministic checks, fault localization and local repair. VisualWebArena and MiniWoB experiments examine execution and reuse within the same benchmark family.
- [ClawTrap: A MITM-Based Red-Teaming Framework for Real-World OpenClaw Security Evaluation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2603-18762/) — 2026-03-19
  Presents a man-in-the-middle network red-teaming framework for evaluating autonomous OpenClaw agents under live traffic manipulation.
- [Why Do LLM-based Web Agents Fail? A Hierarchical Planning Perspective](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2603-14248/) — 2026-03-15
  Presents a hierarchical planning framework for evaluating web agents at planning, execution and replanning stages, and reports low-level execution as the main failure source.
- [Enhancing Web Agents with a Hierarchical Memory Tree](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2603-07024/) — 2026-03-07
  Introduces Hierarchical Memory Tree (HMT), which organizes web-agent trajectories by intent, stage and action, then uses the hierarchy to plan and act on websites.
- [STRUCTUREDAGENT: Planning with AND/OR Trees for Long-Horizon Web Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2603-05294/) — 2026-03-05
  Introduces STRUCTUREDAGENT, a web-agent planning framework that interleaves execution with dynamic AND/OR trees, supporting node repair and exploration of alternative plans.
- [WebFactory: Automated Compression of Foundational Language Intelligence into Grounded Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2603-05044/) — 2026-03-05
  Introduces WebFactory, a GUI-agent training pipeline that synthesizes websites, generates tasks and trajectories, and trains with decomposed rewards. Offline and online transfer evaluations examine agents trained on the generated environments.
- [TimeWarp: Evaluating Web Agents by Revisiting the Past](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2603-04949/) — 2026-03-05
  TimeWarp tests web agents across multiple versions of the same sites, varying visual design, frontend code and workflows. TimeTraj transfers plans across versions to collect training trajectories and study robustness to interface change.
- [Atomicity for Agents: Exposing, Exploiting, and Mitigating TOCTOU Vulnerabilities in Browser-Use Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2603-00476/) — 2026-02-28
  Identifies time-of-check to time-of-use (TOCTOU) race vulnerabilities when web pages mutate between agent planning and action execution.
- [ActionEngine: From Reactive to Programmatic Web Agents via State Machine Memory](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-20502/) — 2026-02-24
  ActionEngine explores web applications to build an updateable state-machine memory of GUI states, operations and transitions. It generates complete task programs from that memory, using reactive repair to handle changed interfaces or missing knowledge.
- [Modeling Distinct Human Interaction in Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-17588/) — 2026-02-19
  CowCorpus records interleaved human and agent actions during web navigation and identifies distinct patterns of user intervention. Models trained to anticipate these interventions are integrated into live web agents and assessed in a user study.
- [Web Agents Should Use Typed Actions Instead of Click-Based Browsing](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-17245/) — 2026-02-19
  Proposes web verbs as typed actions for web agents, exposing operations with structured inputs, outputs and conditions so agents can compose auditable workflows.
- [Persona2Web: Benchmarking Personalized Web Agents for Contextual Reasoning with User History](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-17003/) — 2026-02-19
  Persona2Web tests whether web agents can resolve ambiguous requests using preferences implicit in a user’s history. Its reasoning-aware evaluation compares agent architectures and history-access strategies on personalized tasks on the open web.
- [World-Model-Augmented Web Agents with Action Correction](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-15384/) — 2026-02-17
  WAC has a web-agent action model consult a world model, simulate action outcomes and use judge feedback to correct actions before execution.
- [EmbeWebAgent: Embedding Web Agents into Any Customized UI](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-14865/) — 2026-02-16
  EmbeWebAgent embeds agent access into existing web applications through frontend observations and a page-specific function registry. Its workflow combines GUI primitives, composite actions and MCP tools, demonstrated in a live interface.
- [AutoWebWorld: Synthesizing Infinite Verifiable Web Environments via Finite State Machines](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-14296/) — 2026-02-15
  AutoWebWorld turns finite-state task models into interactive websites whose actions and goal states can be checked programmatically. It generates verified trajectories for training and evaluates transfer to WebVoyager and Online-Mind2Web.
- [OpAgent: Operator Agent for Web Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-13559/) — 2026-02-14
  OpAgent combines multi-task GUI fine-tuning with online web reinforcement learning using outcome judgments and rule-based progress rewards. A modular planner, grounder, reflector and summarizer support navigation and recovery, evaluated separately from the underlying trained policy.
- [SPILLage: Agentic Oversharing on the Web](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-13516/) — 2026-02-13
  Formalizes and measures natural agentic oversharing when browser agents handle private user emails and calendars across live websites.
- [Scaling Web Agent Training through Automatic Data Generation and Fine-grained Evaluation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-12544/) — 2026-02-13
  This work uses constraint-based progress evaluation to retain partially successful web trajectories for training. It introduces BookingArena for structured booking tasks and studies a distilled agent trained with the resulting data.
- [Agentic Test-Time Scaling for WebAgents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-12276/) — 2026-02-12
  CATTS allocates additional inference computation when an agent’s action votes show uncertainty. The paper compares voting and arbiter strategies and evaluates this selective scaling on WebArena-Lite, Online-Mind2Web and GoBrowse.
- [ICA: Information-Aware Credit Assignment for Visually Grounded Long-Horizon Information-Seeking Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-10863/) — 2026-02-11
  Information-Aware Credit Assignment scores intermediate information gathered by web agents, using rendered webpage snapshots to compare evidence across trajectories.
- [MUZZLE: Adaptive Agentic Red-Teaming of Web Agents Against Indirect Prompt Injection Attacks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-09222/) — 2026-02-09
  MUZZLE evaluates web-agent exposure to indirect prompt injection by finding attack surfaces in agent trajectories and refining injected instructions from execution feedback.
- [Do Web Agents Investigate Before They Decide?](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-05354/) — 2026-02-05
  Introduces MIRAGE, a web decision benchmark where agents must actively uncover evidence that can contradict the visible context. Separate investigation, reasoning, decision, and hallucination measures diagnose failures beyond reaching the right page.
- [WebSentinel: Detecting and Localizing Prompt Injection Attacks for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-03792/) — 2026-02-03
  WebSentinel identifies suspicious webpage segments and checks each against its surrounding page context to detect and locate prompt injections. Its evaluation uses both contaminated and clean webpage datasets.
- [Avenir-Web: Human-Experience-Imitating Multimodal Web Agents with Mixture of Grounding Experts](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-02468/) — 2026-02-02
  Avenir-Web combines multiple grounding specialists with procedural experience, a task checklist and adaptive memory. It evaluates this planning and execution framework on live Online-Mind2Web tasks.
- [SimGym: Traffic-Grounded Browser Agents for Offline A/B Testing in E-Commerce](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2602-01443/) — 2026-02-01
  SimGym runs traffic-grounded synthetic buyers as browser agents to compare e-commerce storefront variants offline, using buyer profiles derived from production interactions.
- [DynaWeb: Model-Based Reinforcement Learning of Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2601-22149/) — 2026-01-29
  DynaWeb learns a world model that predicts web-page representations after actions and uses it to generate policy-training rollouts. It interleaves these rollouts with expert trajectories and evaluates the trained web agents on WebArena and WebVoyager.
- [How do Visual Attributes Influence Web Agents? A Comprehensive Evaluation of User Interface Design Factors](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2601-21961/) — 2026-01-29
  Introduces VAF, a controlled pipeline that varies webpage appearance while retaining item semantics. It measures both agent clicks and reasoning references to study which visual design factors change browsing decisions.
- [WebArbiter: A Principle-Guided Reasoning Process Reward Model for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2601-21872/) — 2026-01-29
  WebArbiter generates reasoned action-preference verdicts for web navigation, using reasoning distillation followed by reinforcement learning. It introduces WebPRMBench and evaluates both preference judgments and reward-guided trajectory search.
- [MalURLBench: A Benchmark Evaluating Agents' Vulnerabilities When Processing Web URLs](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2601-18113/) — 2026-01-26
  MalURLBench evaluates how LLM-based web agents handle disguised malicious URLs across attack scenarios; the paper also analyzes attack factors and proposes URLGuard.
- [ExpSeek: Self-Triggered Experience Seeking for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2601-08605/) — 2026-01-13
  ExpSeek uses step-level entropy signals to decide when a web agent should request experience-based guidance. It supplies guidance tailored to the current step and evaluates the intervention across web-agent benchmarks.
- [WebTrap Park: An Automated Platform for Systematic Security Evaluation of Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2601-08406/) — 2026-01-13
  Introduces WebTrap Park, a platform that turns web-agent security risks into executable tasks and assesses concrete interactions with live pages. It compares agent frameworks without requiring modifications to their implementations.
- [Web Agent Agentic Reinforcement Learning Decision Model Under Multi-Cost and Failure Risk](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2610-23857/) — 2026-01-12
  Proposes constrained reinforcement learning for web agents that weighs request costs, latency and failure risk across multi-step website tasks.
- [When Bots Take the Bait: Exposing and Mitigating the Emerging Social Engineering Attack in Web Automation Agent](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2601-07263/) — 2026-01-12
  Studies deceptive social-engineering web patterns targeting browser automation agents and proposes defenses against bait-based manipulation.
- [ColorBrowserAgent: Complex Long-Horizon Browser Agent with Adaptive Knowledge Evolution](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2601-07262/) — 2026-01-12
  ColorBrowserAgent converts human feedback into reusable website knowledge and uses that knowledge to guide progressive context summaries. It evaluates the resulting memory and adaptation mechanisms on long browser workflows.
- [WebGym: Scaling Training Environments for Visual Web Agents with Realistic Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2601-02439/) — 2026-01-05
  WebGym supplies realistic web tasks with rubric-based evaluation and an asynchronous rollout system for reinforcement learning. It studies how training on an agent’s own interaction traces scales with task coverage and transfers to unseen websites.
- [Nested Browser-Use Learning for Agentic Information Seeking](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2512-23647/) — 2025-12-29
  NestBrowse organizes browser actions in a nested framework that separates interaction control from page exploration. It evaluates this design for deep information seeking beyond snippet retrieval and direct URL fetching.
- [DECEPTICON: How Dark Patterns Manipulate Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2512-22894/) — 2025-12-28
  DECEPTICON is an environment of 700 web navigation tasks that isolates dark patterns to measure how deceptive interface designs steer agent actions.
- [WebOperator: Action-Aware Tree Search for Autonomous Agents in Web Environment](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2512-12692/) — 2025-12-14
  WebOperator uses tree search, action filtering, and path checks before replay to explore web tasks and backtrack while accounting for irreversible actions.
- [ceLLMate: Sandboxing Browser AI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2512-12594/) — 2025-12-14
  ceLLMate constrains browser-agent authority through an extension that applies policies at the HTTP layer. It evaluates this sandbox against prompt-injection attacks in WASP, avoiding dependence on brittle low-level click and keystroke policies.
- [Privacy Practices of Browser Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2512-07725/) — 2025-12-08
  Presents a privacy evaluation framework for browser agents covering component weaknesses, website defenses, tracking, privacy-related prompts and personal-data leakage. The framework is applied to deployed browser-agent tools.
- [Permission Manifests for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2601-02371/) — 2025-12-07
  Proposes agent-permissions.json, a website manifest for declaring which interactions web agents may perform, with optional references to APIs.
- [FronTalk: Benchmarking Front-End Development as Conversational Code Generation with Multi-Modal Feedback](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2601-04203/) — 2025-12-05
  FronTalk benchmarks multi-turn front-end code generation from text and visual feedback; a web agent explores generated sites for evaluation and in AceCoder.
- [LegalWebAgent: Empowering Access to Justice via LLM-Based Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2512-04105/) — 2025-11-28
  LegalWebAgent separates user-needs clarification, webpage browsing, and actions such as form completion or scheduling. A 15-task evaluation studies legal-service workflows relevant to Québec civil-law users, using HTML and screenshot observations.
- [MCP vs RAG vs NLWeb vs HTML: A Comparison of the Effectiveness and Efficiency of Different Agent Interfaces to the Web (Technical Report)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2511-23281/) — 2025-11-28
  Compares HTML browsing, RAG, MCP, and NLWeb interfaces for web agents performing product search, comparison, and checkout tasks in four simulated e-shops.
- [Real-Time Procedural Learning From Experience for AI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2511-22074/) — 2025-11-27
  PRAXIS stores action outcomes and retrieves past state-action-result examples to guide agent decisions, with evaluation on the REAL web browsing benchmark.
- [BrowseSafe: Understanding and Preventing Prompt Injection Within AI Browser Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2511-20597/) — 2025-11-25
  BrowseSafe builds a benchmark of prompt injections in realistic HTML for browser agents and evaluates defenses against attacks that can affect agent actions.
- [Fara-7B: An Efficient Agentic Model for Computer Use](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2511-19663/) — 2025-11-24
  Introduces FaraGen synthetic web trajectories, a compact screenshot-based action model, and WebTailBench for underrepresented browser tasks.
- [Building Browser Agents: Architecture, Security, and Practical Solutions](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2511-19477/) — 2025-11-22
  Reports architectural and security lessons from building a browser agent, including hybrid accessibility-tree and visual context, browser tooling and programmatic constraints. A WebGames evaluation accompanies the discussion of reliability and specialization.
- [WebCoach: Self-Evolving Web Agents with Cross-Session Memory Guidance](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2511-12997/) — 2025-11-17
  Introduces WebCoach, which condenses navigation logs into persistent episodic memory and retrieves experiences by similarity and recency. Runtime hooks selectively supply advice to browser agents, evaluated on WebVoyager without retraining the underlying models.
- [AgentPRM: Process Reward Models for LLM Agents via Step-Wise Promise and Progress](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2511-08325/) — 2025-11-11
  AgentPRM scores sequential agent decisions by their promise and progress toward a goal, with web shopping and browser navigation among the motivating tasks.
- [WebVIA: A Web-based Vision-Language Agentic Framework for Interactive and Verifiable UI-to-Code Generation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2511-06251/) — 2025-11-09
  WebVIA combines a web UI exploration agent, interactive UI-to-Code generation, and validation of generated code's interactivity.
- [SynthAgent: Adapting Web Agents with Synthetic Supervision](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2511-06101/) — 2025-11-08
  Introduces SynthAgent, which generates tasks by exploring website elements and refines them when observations reveal conflicts. A later trajectory-refinement stage removes misaligned behavior before fine-tuning agents for the target websites.
- [WebATLAS: An LLM Agent with Experience-Driven Memory and Action Simulation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-22732/) — 2025-10-26
  WebATLAS is a web agent that stores interaction outcomes and simulates candidate actions before acting, using past experience to plan navigation on unseen websites.
- [Branch-and-Browse: Efficient and Controllable Web Exploration with Tree-Structured Reasoning and Action Memory](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-19838/) — 2025-10-18
  Branch-and-Browse is a web agent framework that uses tree-structured subtasks, web state replay, and page action memory to explore and complete tasks.
- [WEBSERV: A Full-Stack and RL-Ready Web Environment for Training Web Agents at Scale](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-16252/) — 2025-10-17
  WebServ combines copy-on-write container environments with a compact DOM-derived browser interface for training web agents. Network-aware waiting supports action execution in single-page applications, with evaluations and reinforcement-learning experiments on WebArena-Lite.
- [PolySkill: Learning Generalizable Skills Through Polymorphic Abstraction](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-15863/) — 2025-10-17
  Introduces PolySkill, which separates a web-agent skill's goal from its execution so agents can learn and reuse skills across websites.
- [WebAggregator: Enhancing Compositional Reasoning Capabilities of Deep Research Agent Foundation Models](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-14438/) — 2025-10-16
  Introduces WebAggregator, a pipeline for synthesizing compositional questions from websites to train deep-research agents, plus the WebAggregatorQA testbed.
- [HackWorld: Evaluating Computer-Use Agents on Exploiting Web Application Vulnerabilities](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-12200/) — 2025-10-14
  Introduces HackWorld for evaluating visual computer-use agents in a capture-the-flag collection of web applications. The benchmark examines interface navigation and multistep planning during vulnerability discovery and exploitation tasks.
- [WebRouter: Query-specific Router via Variational Information Bottleneck for Cost-sensitive Web Agent](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-11221/) — 2025-10-13
  Introduces WebRouter, a cost-aware router that compresses web-agent prompts to choose among LLMs, with cost and accuracy measured on WebVoyager websites.
- [BrowserAgent: Building Web Agents with Human-Inspired Web Browsing Actions](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-10666/) — 2025-10-12
  Trains BrowserAgent to use Playwright-based scrolling, clicking and typing directly on webpages for information-seeking tasks. Supervised and rejection fine-tuning are paired with explicit memory for conclusions carried across steps.
- [WARC-Bench: Web Archive Based Benchmark for GUI Subtask Executions](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-09872/) — 2025-10-10
  WARC-Bench packages dynamic web pages as replayable archives and evaluates short navigation subtasks involving components such as date pickers and scrollable containers. It also studies supervised training followed by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards on these browser interactions.
- [JEF-Hinter: Leveraging Offline Knowledge for Improving Web Agents Adaptation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-04373/) — 2025-10-05
  Introduces JEF-Hinter, which distills successful and failed trajectories into compact episodic hints and retrieves them for the current agent state. Web benchmark experiments examine guidance from decisive steps.
- [Cross-Modal Content Optimization for Steering Web Agent Preferences](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-03612/) — 2025-10-04
  Introduces Cross-Modal Preference Steering (CPS), a black-box attack that edits a listing's images and text to steer VLM-based web agents' selections.
- [BrowserArena: Evaluating LLM Agents on Real-World Web Navigation Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-02418/) — 2025-10-02
  Introduces BrowserArena, a live-web platform for head-to-head agent comparisons on user-submitted tasks. Step-level human annotations support targeted analysis of navigation, pop-up handling and CAPTCHA-related failures.
- [WALT: Web Agents that Learn Tools](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-01524/) — 2025-10-01
  WALT explores website functionality and converts demonstrated interactions into validated, reusable tools combining DOM actions, URL navigation, and agentic fallbacks. It evaluates this expanded browser action space on WebArena and VisualWebArena.
- [Graph2Eval: Automatic Multimodal Task Generation for Agents via Knowledge Graphs](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-00507/) — 2025-10-01
  Introduces Graph2Eval, which generates multimodal agent evaluation tasks from knowledge graphs, including multi-step web interactions in Graph2Eval-Bench.
- [ReasoningBank: Scaling Agent Self-Evolving with Reasoning Memory](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2509-25140/) — 2025-09-29
  Distills generalizable reasoning strategies from both successful and failed agent trajectories into a persistent memory bank for test-time retrieval.
- [Retrieval-augmented GUI Agents with Generative Guidelines](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2509-24183/) — 2025-09-29
  RAG-GUI trains a small guidance adapter to filter and summarize retrieved web tutorials while keeping the acting agent fixed. Supervised and rejection-sampling stages prepare the adapter for inference-time assistance, tested on offline web/mobile tasks and interactive Android tasks.
- [WAREX: Web Agent Reliability Evaluation on Existing Benchmarks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2510-03285/) — 2025-09-28
  WAREX augments existing web-agent benchmarks with reliability challenges arising from unstable systems and changing or disruptive website behavior. Evaluation on WebArena, WebVoyager and REAL examines how these disturbances affect task completion.
- [Can We Stop Malicious AI? KILLBENCH: A Benchmark for External AI Kill Switch Feasibility](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2511-13725/) — 2025-09-26
  KillBench evaluates external prompt-style signals intended to halt maliciously operating web agents, across harmful scenarios and agent configurations.
- [Recon-Act: A Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Browser-Use System via Web Reconnaissance, Tool Generation, and Task Execution](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2509-21072/) — 2025-09-25
  Introduces Recon-Act, a browser-use framework that compares failed and successful web trajectories to create reusable tools for planning and executing long-horizon website tasks.
- [Detecting Pipeline Failures through Fine-Grained Analysis of Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2509-14382/) — 2025-09-17
  Introduces a modular framework for identifying errors at intermediate stages of a web-agent pipeline. A SeeAct and Mind2Web case study examines weaknesses that are obscured by final task-success metrics.
- [TGPO: Tree-Guided Preference Optimization for Robust Web Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2509-14172/) — 2025-09-17
  TGPO merges semantically equivalent web states into tree-structured trajectories for offline preference optimization. A process reward model scores subgoal progress, redundant behavior, and action validity, while dynamic weighting emphasizes consequential decisions.
- [ReSum: Unlocking Long-Horizon Search Intelligence via Context Summarization](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2509-13313/) — 2025-09-16
  Introduces ReSum, which compresses interaction histories during long web searches, and ReSum-GRPO, which trains agents to reason across the resulting segments.
- [PrivWeb: Unobtrusive and Content-aware Privacy Protection For Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2509-11939/) — 2025-09-15
  Introduces PrivWeb, which uses a local language model to anonymize interface information according to user preferences. Adaptive notifications and selective pauses give users control over sensitive information access during web-agent tasks.
- [MMSearch-Plus: Benchmarking Provenance-Aware Search for Multimodal Browsing Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2508-21475/) — 2025-08-29
  Introduces MMSearch-Plus, a multimodal browsing benchmark with iterative image-text search, and an agent framework that marks and crops images for targeted retrieval.
- [Morae: Proactively Pausing UI Agents for User Choices](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2508-21456/) — 2025-08-29
  Introduces Morae, a UI agent that detects decision points and pauses for user choices using interface code and screenshots. A study with blind and low-vision participants evaluates this mixed-initiative approach on real web tasks.
- [WebSight: A Vision-First Architecture for Robust Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2508-16987/) — 2025-08-23
  WebSight uses a vision-language model trained on web-interface examples within a modular agent with planning, action, verification, and episodic memory components. It evaluates element clicking on Showdown Clicks and full browsing tasks on WebVoyager using visual observations.
- [A Functionality-Grounded Benchmark for Evaluating Web Agents in E-commerce Domains](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2508-15832/) — 2025-08-18
  Amazon-Bench generates functionality-specific web tasks from page content and interactive controls, covering operations beyond product search. Its evaluator checks task completion and unintended changes to account state, such as address or purchase settings.
- [WebMall -- A Multi-Shop Benchmark for Evaluating Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2508-13024/) — 2025-08-18
  WebMall provides four simulated online shops with heterogeneous product data for comparison shopping, related-product searches, and checkout tasks. It evaluates web agents with different observation formats and memory configurations in a fixed offline environment.
- [MM-BrowseComp: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Multimodal Browsing Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2508-13186/) — 2025-08-14
  Introduces MM-BrowseComp, a benchmark for browsing agents that must use evidence from web images and videos to answer multimodal search questions.
- [Cognitive Duality for Adaptive Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2508-05081/) — 2025-08-07
  Introduces CogniWeb, which switches between fast reactive processing and slower deliberative reasoning according to task complexity. WebArena experiments examine this combination of learned behaviors and online planning.
- [Beyond Pixels: Exploring DOM Downsampling for LLM-Based Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2508-04412/) — 2025-08-06
  D2Snap reduces serialized DOM observations while preserving actionable elements and features that distinguish their uses. The study compares reduced DOM inputs with screenshot-based observations for a web agent on tasks sampled from Online-Mind2Web.
- [HarmonyGuard: Toward Safety and Utility in Web Agents via Adaptive Policy Enhancement and Dual-Objective Optimization](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2508-04010/) — 2025-08-06
  Introduces HarmonyGuard, a multi-agent framework that updates security policies and weighs safety against task completion for agents operating on the open web.
- [Web-CogReasoner: Towards Multimodal Knowledge-Induced Cognitive Reasoning for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2508-01858/) — 2025-08-03
  Web-CogReasoner separates factual and conceptual web knowledge from procedural reasoning, using a structured curriculum to train a multimodal agent. Web-CogDataset and Web-CogBench support study of these capabilities alongside web-navigation evaluation.
- [General Agentic Planning Through Simulative Reasoning with World Models](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2507-23773/) — 2025-07-31
  Introduces SiRA, an agent architecture that uses a language-based world model to simulate action outcomes for planning in web-browser tasks.
- [Magentic-UI: Towards Human-in-the-loop Agentic Systems](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2507-22358/) — 2025-07-30
  Magentic-UI combines browser interaction, code execution, file operations, and MCP tools in an interface for human-agent collaboration. It studies collaborative planning and task execution, action guards, and persistent memory through benchmark tasks, simulated users, user studies, and safety assessments.
- [Manipulating LLM Web Agents with Indirect Prompt Injection Attack via HTML Accessibility Tree](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2507-14799/) — 2025-07-20
  Studies indirect prompt injection through HTML content consumed as accessibility trees. The evaluation tests how manipulated page observations redirect browser-agent actions on real websites.
- [Let's Think in Two Steps: Mitigating Agreement Bias in MLLMs with Self-Grounded Verification](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2507-11662/) — 2025-07-15
  Studies agreement bias in multimodal agent verifiers across web navigation and computer use, and introduces SGV to assess trajectories using self-generated priors.
- [R-VLM: Region-Aware Vision Language Model for Precise GUI Grounding](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2507-05673/) — 2025-07-08
  R-VLM localizes GUI elements through zoomed-in region proposals and an intersection-over-union-aware training objective. It evaluates the grounding model on ScreenSpot and AgentStudio and studies its effect on navigation in AITW and Mind2Web.
- [WebSynthesis: World-Model-Guided MCTS for Efficient WebUI-Trajectory Synthesis](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2507-04370/) — 2025-07-06
  Introduces WebSynthesis, which uses a learned web world model and tree-based planning to synthesize interaction trajectories for training web agents.
- [Less is More: Empowering GUI Agent with Context-Aware Simplification](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2507-03730/) — 2025-07-04
  SimpAgent prunes distracting interface elements with visual masks and compresses interaction history using consistency-based guidance. The study evaluates these two forms of context reduction during GUI navigation in web and mobile environments.
- [WebArxiv: A Reproducible Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal Web Agents on arXiv Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2507-00938/) — 2025-07-01
  WebArxiv provides 510 static-snapshot tasks with deterministic answers for scholarly browsing, including constrained paper retrieval, content extraction, and cross-paper comparison. The study also tests dynamic retrieval of interaction history to address repetitive or incomplete agent reasoning.
- [LineRetriever: Planning-Aware Observation Reduction for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2507-00210/) — 2025-06-30
  LineRetriever uses a language model to select observation lines that matter for upcoming web-navigation actions. It reduces DOM or accessibility-tree context by considering the planning horizon.
