- [Mind2Web 2: Evaluating Agentic Search with Agent-as-a-Judge](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2506-21506/) — 2025-06-26
  Introduces Mind2Web 2, 130 long-horizon tasks requiring live web browsing and information synthesis, plus an Agent-as-a-Judge framework for grading answers and sources.
- [Embodied Web Agents: Bridging Physical-Digital Realms for Integrated Agent Intelligence](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2506-15677/) — 2025-06-18
  Introduces environments that couple 3D physical-world simulations with functional web interfaces. The accompanying benchmark evaluates tasks requiring agents to coordinate online information and physical actions across cooking, navigation and shopping scenarios.
- [Build the web for agents, not agents for the web](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2506-10953/) — 2025-06-12
  Proposes Agentic Web Interface (AWI), a website interface intended for agent navigation, and sets out six design principles for web-agent use.
- [Mirage-1: Augmenting and Updating GUI Agent with Hierarchical Multimodal Skills](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2506-10387/) — 2025-06-12
  Mirage-1 organizes interaction trajectories into execution, core, and meta-skills, then uses them to guide Monte Carlo tree search during online GUI tasks. It introduces AndroidLH for long-horizon evaluation and tests the approach on Android and web-navigation benchmarks.
- [EconWebArena: Benchmarking Autonomous Agents on Economic Tasks in Realistic Web Environments](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2506-08136/) — 2025-06-09
  Introduces EconWebArena, a curated benchmark of economic-data tasks requiring live website navigation, multimodal interpretation, and precise extraction. It evaluates visual grounding, planning, and interaction design within multistep workflows.
- [Thinking vs. Doing: Agents that Reason by Scaling Test-Time Interaction](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2506-07976/) — 2025-06-09
  Test-Time Interaction scales the interaction horizon so web agents can explore, backtrack and revise plans within a rollout. Its online reinforcement-learning curriculum adjusts rollout lengths and is evaluated on WebVoyager and WebArena.
- [Go-Browse: Training Web Agents with Structured Exploration](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2506-03533/) — 2025-06-04
  Go-Browse frames web-environment exploration as graph search so that information can be reused across data-collection episodes. It collects 10,000 successful trajectories and 40,000 interaction steps on WebArena and uses them to fine-tune a web agent.
- [Surfer-H Meets Holo1: Cost-Efficient Web Agent Powered by Open Weights](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2506-02865/) — 2025-06-03
  Describes Surfer-H, a web agent paired with the Holo1 vision-language model family trained on web content, synthetic examples, and agent-generated data. The work introduces WebClick for web-interface localization and evaluates browser task execution on WebVoyager.
- [DeepShop: A Benchmark for Deep Research Shopping Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2506-02839/) — 2025-06-03
  DeepShop evaluates shopping agents with evolving query complexity across product attributes, filters and sorting preferences. Its evaluation combines checks of these individual requirements with overall task success.
- [WebChoreArena: Evaluating Web Browsing Agents on Realistic Tedious Web Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2506-01952/) — 2025-06-02
  Introduces WebChoreArena, 532 web chores built on WebArena environments to evaluate agents’ retention of page information, calculation, and tracking across pages.
- [ResTest: Targeted Coverage of Residual Not-Covered Code Using Large Language Models for Web GUI Testing](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2506-00520/) — 2025-05-31
  Introduces ResTest, which uses coverage reports and a state transition graph to infer untested web functions, then directs a GUI agent to execute them.
- [Open CaptchaWorld: A Comprehensive Web-based Platform for Testing and Benchmarking Multimodal LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2505-24878/) — 2025-05-30
  Open CaptchaWorld evaluates multimodal agents on interactive browser-based CAPTCHA puzzles. Its benchmark distinguishes task success from the depth of visual reasoning and interaction needed to complete a puzzle.
- [AdInject: Real-World Black-Box Attacks on Web Agents via Advertising Delivery](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2505-21499/) — 2025-05-27
  Studies advertising delivery as an indirect-injection channel for web agents under a black-box threat model. AdInject evaluates how static ad content shaped by website context can redirect browser actions.
- [WebCoT: Enhancing Web Agent Reasoning by Reconstructing Chain-of-Thought in Reflection, Branching, and Rollback](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2505-20013/) — 2025-05-26
  WebCoT turns reflection, look-ahead, branching and rollback procedures into chain-of-thought training rationales. Fine-tuning on these reconstructed trajectories is evaluated for web-agent self-improvement and transfer across navigation tasks.
- [WebAgent-R1: Training Web Agents via End-to-End Multi-Turn Reinforcement Learning](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2505-16421/) — 2025-05-22
  Trains web agents through asynchronous multi-turn environment interaction, using task-success rewards to optimize complete trajectories.
- [Web-Shepherd: Advancing PRMs for Reinforcing Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2505-15277/) — 2025-05-21
  Web-Shepherd is a process reward model for assessing individual steps of web-navigation trajectories. It introduces a collection of 40,000 step-level preference pairs with checklists and a separate benchmark for comparing reward models, then evaluates verification during WebArena-lite navigation.
- [The Hidden Dangers of Browsing AI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2505-13076/) — 2025-05-19
  Outlines an end-to-end threat model and empirical security evaluation across architectural layers of autonomous browser agents.
- [WebInject: Prompt Injection Attack to Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2505-11717/) — 2025-05-16
  Introduces WebInject, a prompt-injection attack that optimizes rendered webpage pixels to make screenshot-based web agents take attacker-chosen actions.
- [AgentVigil: Generic Black-Box Red-teaming for Indirect Prompt Injection against LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2505-05849/) — 2025-05-09
  Introduces AgentVigil, a black-box fuzzing framework for finding indirect prompt injections against LLM agents, including attacks that redirect web navigation.
- [WebGen-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Generating Interactive and Functional Websites from Scratch](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2505-03733/) — 2025-05-06
  Introduces WebGen-Bench for evaluating agents that generate multi-file websites, using a web-navigation agent to check whether the resulting pages pass interaction tests.
- [Grounding Multimodal Large Language Model in GUI World](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-dca82e9214c5926109f1/) — 2025-05-01
  Trains a 0.4B SAM-initialized GUI grounding model on 0.5M auto-collected web screenshots with 35M element annotations, then links it to a Qwen-VL planner through a position token for MiniWob, AITW and Mind2Web.
- [Learning to Contextualize Web Pages for Enhanced Decision Making by LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-60a2dc930fa11860858a/) — 2025-05-01
  Trains a separate language model to rewrite lengthy web page observations into focused descriptions for LLM web agents, keeping training targets that let several LLMs predict the correct next action.
- [Visual Test-time Scaling for GUI Agent Grounding](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2505-00684/) — 2025-05-01
  RegionFocus adds visual test-time scaling by zooming into selected screenshot regions and maintaining an image-based map of landmarks and actions. It evaluates this grounding procedure with UI-TARS and Qwen2.5-VL on ScreenSpot-Pro and WebVoyager.
- [WebThinker: Empowering Large Reasoning Models with Deep Research Capability](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2504-21776/) — 2025-04-30
  Combines iterative web search and link-following with interleaved reasoning and report drafting for deep research. Its reported web explorer retrieves page content through a crawler.
- [WebEvolver: Enhancing Web Agent Self-Improvement with Coevolving World Model](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2504-21024/) — 2025-04-23
  WebEvolver trains a co-evolving world model to predict web observations and generate additional agent-training trajectories. The same model supports look-ahead action selection at inference, with evaluation on live web-task benchmarks.
- [WASP: Benchmarking Web Agent Security Against Prompt Injection Attacks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2504-18575/) — 2025-04-22
  Introduces WASP, an end-to-end benchmark for prompt-injection security in web agents. Its evaluation separates partial attack progress from completion of the attacker’s full objective.
- [WebLists: Extracting Structured Information From Complex Interactive Websites Using Executable LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2504-12682/) — 2025-04-17
  Introduces WebLists, a benchmark for structured extraction from interactive websites, and BardeenAgent, which turns web-agent runs into replayable CSS-selector programs.
- [WebRollback: Enhancing Web Agents with Explicit Rollback Mechanisms](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2504-11788/) — 2025-04-16
  WebRollback gives a web agent an explicit action for returning to an earlier state in its navigation trajectory. It studies this recovery mechanism on two live web-navigation benchmarks under both zero-shot and fine-tuned settings.
- [REAL: Benchmarking Autonomous Agents on Deterministic Simulations of Real Websites](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2504-11543/) — 2025-04-15
  Introduces REAL, a benchmark of deterministic website replicas with 112 tasks covering information retrieval and state-changing browser actions.
- [AgentA/B: Automated and Scalable Web A/BTesting with Interactive LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2504-09723/) — 2025-04-13
  Introduces AgentA/B, which uses persona-based LLM agents to navigate real webpages and perform shopping interactions for simulated web A/B tests.
- [AgentRewardBench: Evaluating Automatic Evaluations of Web Agent Trajectories](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2504-08942/) — 2025-04-11
  AgentRewardBench evaluates automated web-agent judges against expert reviews of 1,302 trajectories from five benchmarks. The annotations cover completion, side effects, and repetition, enabling comparison of LLM judges and rule-based task evaluators.
- [SkillWeaver: Web Agents can Self-Improve by Discovering and Honing Skills](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2504-07079/) — 2025-04-09
  SkillWeaver explores websites, practices interactions, and converts the resulting experience into a reusable library of callable skill APIs. Its WebArena and live-website experiments examine skill refinement, composition, and transfer from stronger agents to weaker ones.
- [Inducing Programmatic Skills for Agentic Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2504-06821/) — 2025-04-09
  Introduces agent skill induction (ASI), which creates, checks, and reuses program-based skills for web tasks by combining primitive actions into higher-level routines.
- [Are Autonomous Web Agents Good Testers?](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2504-01495/) — 2025-04-02
  Adapts web agents to execute natural-language test steps, verify assertions, and issue test verdicts. The study introduces 113 passing and failing test cases across three offline web applications and compares two agent implementations with qualitative failure analysis.
- [An Illusion of Progress? Assessing the Current State of Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2504-01382/) — 2025-04-02
  Introduces Online-Mind2Web, with 300 tasks across 136 live websites, and examines how benchmark design affects assessments of web agents. It also develops an LLM judge for task completion and compares its judgments with human evaluation.
- [A Survey of WebAgents: Towards Next-Generation AI Agents for Web Automation with Large Foundation Models](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2503-23350/) — 2025-03-30
  Surveys foundation-model web agents through their architectures, training methods and trustworthiness. It organizes research on completing browser tasks from user instructions and identifies directions for further development.
- [WebNav: An Intelligent Agent for Voice-Controlled Web Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2503-13843/) — 2025-03-18
  Introduces WebNav, a web navigation agent that turns natural-language commands into GUI actions using screenshot context, DOM labels, and separate LLMs for planning and action parameters.
- [Towards Ethical and Personalized Web Navigation Agents: A Framework for User-Aligned Task Execution](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-2af250798e99e7af01ce/) — 2025-03-10
  A web navigation framework uses user-specific task representations and interactive reasoning to adapt agent workflows to preferences, with task success and satisfaction as evaluation measures.
- [BEARCUBS: A benchmark for computer-using web agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2503-07919/) — 2025-03-10
  BEARCUBS provides 111 live-web information-seeking questions with short answers and human-validated browsing trajectories. Its tasks require multimodal computer interaction, including video understanding and navigation of interactive 3D content.
- [LiteWebAgent: The Open-Source Suite for VLM-Based Web-Agent Applications](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2503-02950/) — 2025-03-04
  Introduces a modular visual web-agent stack with planning and memory components, exposed through a remote-browser application and Chrome extension.
- [Why Are Web AI Agents More Vulnerable Than Standalone LLMs? A Security Analysis](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2502-20383/) — 2025-02-27
  Analyzes web-agent vulnerabilities at the component level, examining goal placement in prompts, multistep action generation, and observations as sources of increased susceptibility.
- [WebGames: Challenging General-Purpose Web-Browsing AI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2502-18356/) — 2025-02-25
  Introduces WebGames, a browser-agent benchmark with 50+ interactive challenges in a self-contained test environment and verifiable solutions.
- [UXAgent: An LLM Agent-Based Usability Testing Framework for Web Design](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2502-12561/) — 2025-02-18
  Introduces UXAgent, which uses simulated users and a browser connector to test website usability and present their actions, responses, and recordings to UX researchers.
- [Explorer: Scaling Exploration-driven Web Trajectory Synthesis for Multimodal Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2502-11357/) — 2025-02-17
  Synthesizes multimodal web trajectories through exploration and refinement, then trains a browser agent to study the effect of scaling interaction data.
- [Symbiotic Cooperation for Web Agents: Harnessing Complementary Strengths of Large and Small LLMs](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2502-07942/) — 2025-02-11
  Couples large- and small-model web-agent exploration with trajectory synthesis and distillation to improve both model types iteratively.
- [InSTA: Towards Internet-Scale Training For Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2502-06776/) — 2025-02-10
  InSTA builds a pipeline that creates website tasks, collects web-agent trajectories and filters them by judged success to train smaller web navigation agents.
- [CowPilot: A Framework for Autonomous and Human-Agent Collaborative Web Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2501-16609/) — 2025-01-28
  Introduces CowPilot, a web-navigation framework where agents suggest actions and users can pause, reject, override, or resume them. It evaluates autonomous and collaborative use through task success and human effort.
- [Operator System Card](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-24bc39ed487b311108ae/) — 2025-01-23
  The Operator system card describes a browser agent trained to act from screenshots and details evaluations, risk policies, user confirmations, and monitoring for web tasks.
- [R2D2: Remembering, Replaying and Dynamic Decision Making with a Reflective Agentic Memory](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2501-12485/) — 2025-01-21
  R2D2 gives web agents a replay-buffer map of visited pages and a reflection step for analyzing errors and revising navigation strategies.
- [WebWalker: Benchmarking LLMs in Web Traversal](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2501-07572/) — 2025-01-13
  Introduces WebWalkerQA and an exploration-and-critique framework for gathering information by traversing multiple levels of a website.
- [MemAgent: A cache-inspired framework for augmenting conversational Web Agents with task-specific information](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-3fdea133115518f9205a/) — 2025
  MemAgent adds a cache for task-specific information to conversational web agents, separating information gathering from execution across 150 web tasks.
- [Proposer-Agent-Evaluator(PAE): Autonomous Skill Discovery For Foundation Model Internet Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2412-13194/) — 2024-12-17
  Combines autonomous task proposal, web interaction, and visual success evaluation to discover skills and improve browser-agent policies.
- [WEPO: Web Element Preference Optimization for LLM-based Web Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2412-10742/) — 2024-12-14
  Uses web-element preference optimization with sampled negative HTML elements to align high-level user intent with navigation actions.
- [AgentTrek: Agent Trajectory Synthesis via Guiding Replay with Web Tutorials](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2412-09605/) — 2024-12-12
  Converts public tutorials into task specifications and executes them with an agent to synthesize multimodal interaction trajectories.
- [ScribeAgent: Towards Specialized Web Agents Using Production-Scale Workflow Data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2411-15004/) — 2024-11-22
  Studies fine-tuning open language models on production web-workflow traces, including training choices for specialized web agents.
- [AdaptAgent: Adapting Multimodal Web Agents with Few-Shot Learning from Human Demonstrations](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2411-13451/) — 2024-11-20
  Adapts multimodal web agents to unfamiliar websites using a small number of human demonstrations through prompting or meta-learning.
- [Generalist Virtual Agents: A Survey on Autonomous Agents Across Digital Platforms](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2411-10943/) — 2024-11-17
  Surveys virtual agents for browsers, applications and operating systems, organizing prior work by task type, observation and action spaces, model strategies and evaluation methods. It examines screen-based interaction and keyboard, mouse and touch control, alongside transferability and long-sequence decision-making challenges.
- [WebRL: Training LLM Web Agents via Self-Evolving Online Curriculum Reinforcement Learning](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2411-02337/) — 2024-11-04
  Trains web agents with an evolving task curriculum, outcome supervision, and reinforcement-learning updates intended to manage policy drift.
- [WebOlympus: An Open Platform for Web Agents on Live Websites](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-07e274077af6ea9fd5f9/) — 2024-11
  Presents WebOlympus, an open platform that runs web agents on live websites from a few lines of code or a Chrome extension, with a safety monitor that blocks harmful actions through human or model checks.
- [From Context to Action: Analysis of the Impact of State Representation and Context on the Generalization of Multi-Turn Web Navigation Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2410-23555/) — 2024-10-31
  Analyzes how interaction history and webpage representation affect multiturn web-agent generalization across unfamiliar websites, task categories, and geographic settings.
- [Auto-Intent: Automated Intent Discovery and Self-Exploration for Large Language Model Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2410-22552/) — 2024-10-29
  Discovers compact intents from demonstrations and uses intent predictions to guide a language-model web agent's decisions and exploration.
- [OpenWebVoyager: Building Multimodal Web Agents via Iterative Real-World Exploration, Feedback and Optimization](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2410-19609/) — 2024-10-25
  Develops multimodal browser agents through repeated cycles of real-web exploration, trajectory feedback, and policy optimization.
- [Infogent: An Agent-Based Framework for Web Information Aggregation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2410-19054/) — 2024-10-24
  Infogent separates navigation, extraction and aggregation for web information gathering, including browser interaction through webpage screenshots.
- [Large Language Models Empowered Personalized Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2410-17236/) — 2024-10-22
  PersonalWAB benchmarks personalized web tasks, while PUMA retrieves relevant past web behavior to adapt agents' instruction handling and actions.
- [Beyond Browsing: API-Based Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2410-16464/) — 2024-10-21
  Compares browsing-only, API-only, and hybrid web agents on WebArena, examining how access to application APIs changes task execution alongside browser interaction.
- [AgentOccam: A Simple Yet Strong Baseline for LLM-Based Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2410-13825/) — 2024-10-17
  Studies how simplifying and aligning a web agent's observations and actions with language-model capabilities affects task completion.
- [NNetNav: Unsupervised Learning of Browser Agents Through Environment Interaction in the Wild](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2410-02907/) — 2024-10-03
  Generates browser-agent demonstrations through autonomous exploration and retrospective task labeling, pruning trajectories that lack meaningful subtasks.
- [ExACT: Teaching AI Agents to Explore with Reflective-MCTS and Exploratory Learning](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2410-02052/) — 2024-10-02
  ExACT combines reflective tree search with learning from search experience to help web agents explore, assess states and backtrack on VisualWebArena.
- [Multimodal Auto Validation For Self-Refinement in Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2410-00689/) — 2024-10-01
  Studies text and visual validation for web agents and uses the resulting validator to detect failures and guide workflow self-refinement.
- [Synatra: Turning Indirect Knowledge into Direct Demonstrations for Digital Agents at Scale](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2409-15637/) — 2024-09-24
  Synatra converts indirect procedural knowledge, including human-oriented tutorials, into direct demonstrations for digital agents. It trains a 7B CodeLlama on 100,000 synthesized demonstrations and evaluates the resulting policy on Mind2Web, MiniWoB++, and WebArena.
- [Steward: Natural Language Web Automation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2409-15441/) — 2024-09-23
  Introduces Steward, a natural-language browser automation system that repeatedly plans and executes website actions. The paper examines completion detection, state representation, responsiveness, and caching in real-site tasks.
- [EIA: Environmental Injection Attack on Generalist Web Agents for Privacy Leakage](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2409-11295/) — 2024-09-17
  Evaluates privacy-directed webpage instruction injection against generalist web agents using PII-related interaction steps from Mind2Web, including attempts to expose specific user information or entire requests.
- [NaviQAte: Functionality-Guided Web Application Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2409-10741/) — 2024-09-16
  Frames web-application exploration as functionality-oriented question answering, generating navigation actions from multimodal context.
- [Agent Workflow Memory](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2409-07429/) — 2024-09-11
  Agent Workflow Memory extracts reusable task routines and selectively supplies them to guide later web actions. It studies both offline workflow induction from training examples and online induction from test experience on Mind2Web and WebArena.
- [WebPilot: A Versatile and Autonomous Multi-Agent System for Web Task Execution with Strategic Exploration](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2408-15978/) — 2024-08-28
  WebPilot separates global subtask planning from local Monte Carlo tree search during web execution. It continually refines the high-level plan and adapts local search to uncertain transitions and incomplete observations, evaluated on WebArena and MiniWoB++.
- [Agent Q: Advanced Reasoning and Learning for Autonomous AI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2408-07199/) — 2024-08-13
  Agent Q combines guided tree search, self-critique and preference-based fine-tuning to train web agents from successful and unsuccessful interactions.
- [OpenWebAgent: An Open Toolkit to Enable Web Agents on Large Language Models](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-6e436831f76a1337ea8b/) — 2024-08
  Presents OpenWebAgent, a web automation toolkit that pairs a Chrome plugin with an HTML parser and an action generation module, in a modular framework where developers plug in LLMs or LMMs.
- [MindSearch: Mimicking Human Minds Elicits Deep AI Searcher](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2407-20183/) — 2024-07-29
  MindSearch uses a planner and searcher agents to split information requests into subquestions and gather answers across web pages.
- [AssistantBench: Can Web Agents Solve Realistic and Time-Consuming Tasks?](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2407-15711/) — 2024-07-22
  Introduces realistic open-web tasks and the SeePlanAct agent, analyzing the limitations of browsing and retrieval in time-consuming assistant work.
- [Agent-E: From Autonomous Web Navigation to Foundational Design Principles in Agentic Systems](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2407-13032/) — 2024-07-17
  Agent-E combines hierarchical web-task control with DOM distillation and observations of interface changes. WebVoyager experiments inform a broader discussion of primitive skills, observation reduction, and self-improvement in agent architecture.
- [Tree Search for Language Model Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2407-01476/) — 2024-07-01
  Applies best-first search to agent actions within interactive web environments, allowing exploration and multistep planning at inference time. WebArena and VisualWebArena experiments examine search behavior and test-time computation.
- [RealWeb: A Benchmark for Universal Instruction Following in Realistic Web Services Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-a998ab1efc741ba69d82/) — 2024-07
  Introduces RealWeb, a Chinese benchmark of 11,739 instructions on 40 real websites with annotated screenshots and slot trees, plus a baseline that detects page regions, parses instructions and acts on the pages.
- [WebCanvas: Benchmarking Web Agents in Online Environments](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2406-12373/) — 2024-06-18
  Introduces WebCanvas and Mind2Web-Live, evaluating online task progress through meaningful intermediate states on changing websites.
- [CAAP: Context-Aware Action Planning Prompting to Solve Computer Tasks with Front-End UI Only](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2406-06947/) — 2024-06-11
  CAAP converts screenshots into textual observations and plans keyboard-and-mouse actions using a prompt that examines task context from multiple perspectives. The agent is evaluated on MiniWoB++ and WebShop using only front-end visual observations.
- [WebSuite: Systematically Evaluating Why Web Agents Fail](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2406-01623/) — 2024-06-01
  WebSuite organizes browser operations into an action taxonomy and tests both isolated controls and complete workflows. Its diagnostic tasks attribute failures to specific actions, enabling comparison of text-based and multimodal web agents.
- [Large Language Models Can Self-Improve At Web Agent Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2405-20309/) — 2024-05-30
  Examines self-improvement by fine-tuning web agents on three mixtures of model-generated training data. WebArena experiments and trajectory-level evaluation measures study task performance, robustness, and behavior beyond aggregate success rates.
- [Navigating WebAI: Training Agents to Complete Web Tasks with Large Language Models and Reinforcement Learning](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2405-00516/) — 2024-05-01
  Combines supervised and reinforcement learning for MiniWoB web tasks and investigates whether agents understand HTML structure or memorize target elements.
- [Grounded Language Agent for Product Search via Intelligent Web Interactions](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2404-10887/) — 2024-04-16
  Introduces GLAINTEL, a Flan-T5-based product-search web agent studied under unsupervised learning, demonstration-based training, and unsupervised domain adaptation. It compares ways to combine demonstrations with reinforcement learning.
- [WILBUR: Adaptive In-Context Learning for Robust and Accurate Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2404-05902/) — 2024-04-08
  WILBUR learns to rank prior task demonstrations for a web agent’s prompt and synthesizes instructions from past runs. A generated task curriculum and a backtracking mechanism support adaptation and recovery, evaluated on WebVoyager.
- [AutoWebGLM: A Large Language Model-based Web Navigating Agent](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2404-03648/) — 2024-04-04
  Builds a web agent through simplified HTML observations, human-and-AI browsing data, curriculum training, and reinforcement-learning refinement.
- [Tur\[k\]ingBench: A Challenge Benchmark for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2403-11905/) — 2024-03-18
  Evaluates web agents on multimodal annotation tasks adapted from HTML interfaces originally built for crowdsourcing workers.
- [AutoGuide: Automated Generation and Selection of Context-Aware Guidelines for Large Language Model Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2403-08978/) — 2024-03-13
  Introduces AutoGuide, which generates conditional, context-aware guidelines from past agent experience to support decisions in sequential tasks, including web navigation.
- [WIPI: A New Web Threat for LLM-Driven Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2402-16965/) — 2024-02-26
  Studies WIPI, a black-box indirect-instruction threat in which webpage content redirects LLM-driven web agents. Evaluation spans plugin-based agents, Web GPTs, and open-source web-agent implementations.
- [On the Multi-turn Instruction Following for Conversational Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2402-15057/) — 2024-02-23
  MT-Mind2Web evaluates web navigation through sequences of conversational user instructions. Self-MAP combines memory-augmented planning with reflection to preserve context and dependencies across those interaction turns.
- [WebLINX: Real-World Website Navigation with Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2402-05930/) — 2024-02-08
  Collects expert demonstrations of conversational web navigation and studies retrieval-based element selection for processing large pages.
- [Dual-View Visual Contextualization for Web Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2402-04476/) — 2024-02-06
  Uses screenshot geometry and visual content to enrich HTML element representations for web navigation. Nearby elements contribute textual and visual context, with evaluation on cross-task, cross-website, and cross-domain Mind2Web splits.
- [WebVoyager: Building an End-to-End Web Agent with Large Multimodal Models](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2401-13919/) — 2024-01-25
  Combines multimodal web interaction with a real-website task collection and an automated evaluation protocol.
- [VisualWebArena: Evaluating Multimodal Agents on Realistic Visual Web Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2401-13649/) — 2024-01-24
  Introduces visually grounded website tasks for evaluating multimodal agents that must interpret both images and text.
- [AgentBoard: An Analytical Evaluation Board of Multi-turn LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2401-13178/) — 2024-01-24
  Introduces AgentBoard, a multiturn agent evaluation framework with intermediate progress metrics and analysis tools. Its broader task suite includes WebShop and WebArena interactions with stepwise progress measurements.
- [GPT-4V(ision) is a Generalist Web Agent, if Grounded](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2401-01614/) — 2024-01-03
  Introduces SeeAct and live-website evaluation, examining how visual planning and element grounding affect web-agent task completion.
- [WebVLN: Vision-and-Language Navigation on Websites](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2312-15820/) — 2023-12-25
  WebVLN trains website navigation from question-based instructions using both rendered visual information and underlying HTML. It introduces WebVLN-v1 and a website-aware navigation network to study this digital variant of vision-and-language navigation.
- [Exposing Limitations of Language Model Agents in Sequential-Task Compositions on the Web](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2311-18751/) — 2023-11-30
  CompWoB adds 50 composed web-automation tasks to study generalization from simpler interactions. The paper compares prompted and fine-tuned agents, balances task training data, and probes sensitivity to changes in instruction order.
- [WebWISE: Web Interface Control and Sequential Exploration with Large Language Models](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2310-16042/) — 2023-10-24
  WebWISE filters DOM elements into observations and generates short programs for click, scroll, and text-entry steps. It evaluates in-context learning from either one supplied example or an example collected during a successful zero-shot MiniWoB++ trial.
- [AllTogether: Investigating the Efficacy of Spliced Prompt for Web Navigation using Large Language Models](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2310-18331/) — 2023-10-20
  AllTogether standardizes prompts for HTML-based web navigation and compares prompting with instruction fine-tuning. Its analysis examines how HTML context, interaction history, and current environmental feedback affect task execution.
- [OpenAgents: An Open Platform for Language Agents in the Wild](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2310-10634/) — 2023-10-16
  OpenAgents hosts three language agents, for data analysis, API plugins and autonomous web browsing, behind a web interface for non-expert users, with local deployment for developers.
- [A Zero-Shot Language Agent for Computer Control with Structured Reflection](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2310-08740/) — 2023-10-12
  Studies a zero-shot computer-control agent that plans executable actions and organizes its own reflections after mistakes. MiniWoB++ experiments examine learning to complete tasks without supplied expert trajectories.
- [SteP: Stacked LLM Policies for Web Actions](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2310-03720/) — 2023-10-05
  SteP dynamically composes specialized web-action policies through a stack that records the active chain of policy calls. It studies this control structure across WebArena, MiniWoB++, and CRM workflows.
- [LASER: LLM Agent with State-Space Exploration for Web Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2309-08172/) — 2023-09-15
  LASER represents web navigation as exploration over a defined set of states, allowing an agent to revisit earlier states after mistakes. The study evaluates this backtracking approach on WebShop and Amazon tasks.
- [GUI-Based Software Testing: An Automated Approach Using GPT-4 and Selenium WebDriver](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-ffc05c4425f777924940/) — 2023-09-11
  A web GUI testing method combines GPT-4 with Selenium WebDriver and compares its branch coverage with random monkey testing on a web calculator.
- [WebArena: A Realistic Web Environment for Building Autonomous Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2307-13854/) — 2023-07-25
  Introduces reproducible web environments with task-completion checks for evaluating autonomous web agents.
- [A Real-World WebAgent with Planning, Long Context Understanding, and Program Synthesis](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2307-12856/) — 2023-07-24
  Combines task decomposition, HTML summarization, and generated Python programs to carry out instructions on websites.
- [Synapse: Trajectory-as-Exemplar Prompting with Memory for Computer Control](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2306-07863/) — 2023-06-13
  Synapse filters task-irrelevant page state and places complete abstracted state-action trajectories into the prompt. An embedding-based exemplar memory retrieves relevant demonstrations for new tasks, evaluated on MiniWoB++ and Mind2Web.
- [Mind2Web: Towards a Generalist Agent for the Web](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2306-06070/) — 2023-06-09
  Introduces a web-task dataset spanning diverse websites and evaluates generalization to unseen sites and domains.
- [From Pixels to UI Actions: Learning to Follow Instructions via Graphical User Interfaces](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2306-00245/) — 2023-05-31
  Studies instruction-following agents that observe screenshots and act through a generic mouse-and-keyboard action space, evaluating pixel-based pretraining for GUI tasks on MiniWoB++.
- [Hierarchical Prompting Assists Large Language Model on Web Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2305-14257/) — 2023-05-23
  Separates web navigation prompting into a SUMMARIZER that extracts action-relevant observations and an ACTOR that chooses the next action from the condensed context.
- [Multimodal Web Navigation with Instruction-Finetuned Foundation Models](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2305-11854/) — 2023-05-19
  Trains WebGUM offline from demonstrations by jointly tuning an instruction-following language model and a vision encoder over webpage screenshots and HTML. The agent predicts browser actions and is evaluated on MiniWoB, WebShop, and Mind2Web.
- [Language Models can Solve Computer Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2303-17491/) — 2023-03-30
  Introduces recursive criticism and improvement prompting for language-model agents performing computer tasks, evaluated on MiniWoB++ with a small number of demonstrations per task.
- [Understanding HTML with Large Language Models](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2210-03945/) — 2022-10-08
  Studies language models for HTML element classification, page description and autonomous web navigation. Fine-tuned models are evaluated on MiniWoB, and a distilled, automatically labeled CommonCrawl HTML corpus supports the training approach.
- [WebShop: Towards Scalable Real-World Web Interaction with Grounded Language Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2207-01206/) — 2022-07-04
  Provides a simulated shopping environment where language agents search, configure, and select products from natural-language requirements.
- [WebGPT: Browser-assisted question-answering with human feedback](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2112-09332/) — 2021-12-17
  Trains a question-answering model to search and navigate a text-based web browser, collect references and improve long-form answers using demonstrations and human feedback.
- [Grounding Open-Domain Instructions to Automate Web Support Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-2103-16057/) — 2021-03-30
  Introduces RUSS, which translates support instructions into a domain-specific language and grounds them in actionable web elements.
- [DOM-Q-NET: Grounded RL on Structured Language](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-1902-07257/) — 2019-02-19
  Introduces DOM-Q-NET, an RL architecture for web navigation that represents HTML as a tree and uses separate Q-function networks for clicking elements and typing text.
- [Learning to Navigate the Web](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-1812-09195/) — 2018-12-21
  Introduces guided RL for QWeb, using a curriculum of smaller instructions and generated tasks to train an agent that follows instructions and fills forms on web pages.
- [Rousillon: Scraping Distributed Hierarchical Web Data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-97c0b1896525632434e2/) — 2018
  Introduces Rousillon, which generalizes a demonstration of collecting one hierarchical data row into a browser-automation program that traverses related pages. The paper develops relation-selection and generalization methods and evaluates users building such scripts.
- [World of Bits: An Open-Domain Platform for Web-Based Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-52a3261eb6a19b402e88/) — 2017-07-17
  Introduces World of Bits, a web-task platform combining human demonstrations, keyboard and mouse actions, and cached HTTP traffic for repeatable task environments. It evaluates agents trained through behavioral cloning and reinforcement learning.
- [Ringer: Web Automation by Demonstration](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-347925d0f440d6396080/) — 2016-11
  Introduces Ringer, which converts demonstrated webpage interactions into scripts combining actions, target selectors, and waiting conditions. It evaluates replay robustness on tasks recorded from real websites.
- [End-to-End Goal-Driven Web Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/arxiv-1602-02261/) — 2016-02-06
  Introduces WebNav for converting websites into goal-directed hyperlink navigation tasks and WikiNav for evaluating language-conditioned navigation and question answering on Wikipedia.
- [Koala: Capture, Share, Automate, Personalize Business Processes on the Web](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-cccd967aa993404d74d0/) — 2007
  Introduces Koala, a collaborative web automation system that records browser interactions as editable pseudo-natural-language scripts. Scripts are shared through a wiki and instantiated with personal or corporate data for reuse by other users.
- [PLOW: A Collaborative Task Learning Agent](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-628b5251757bb3888e89/) — 2007
  Describes PLOW, which learns executable web-task models from a session combining demonstrations, natural-language explanations, and dialogue. Browser instrumentation connects user actions with DOM structure to support task generalization.

## 评测基准

- [Action Check WebMCP](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-ajaknumber4-action-check-webmcp/) — Date not recorded
  External state-verification benchmark testing whether WebMCP browser tools actually produced their claimed DOM and backend effects.
- [agent-bench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-megaurav4-agent-bench/) — Date not recorded
  agent-bench evaluates browser-based AI agents across 50 web tasks spanning navigation, form filling, search, and e-commerce checkout.
- [Agent-CE](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-anaishowland-agent-ce/) — Date not recorded
  Agent-CE is a containerized continuous evaluation platform for benchmarking web browsing agents across reproducible task suites.
- [Agent-friendly UI](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-showlab-aui/) — Date not recorded
  An evaluation and revision pipeline tests generated websites with computer-use agents, checks task completion against rules, and summarizes navigation failures.
- [Agentalyze](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-ilyat9-agentalyze/) — Date not recorded
  Agentalyze is an evaluation harness for LLM browser agents with real Chromium tasks, DOM-based state verifiers, and step-level trace analysis.
- [agentB](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-beme08-agentb/) — Date not recorded
  agentB is a benchmark for evaluating open web agents on realistic knowledge-work and browser automation tasks.
- [AgentBoard](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-hkust-nlp-agentboard/) — Date not recorded
  AgentBoard evaluates multiturn agents across several tasks, including web interactions, and provides progress metrics and visualizations for analyzing their runs.
- [AgentGauntlet](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-konuktor-agent-gauntlet/) — Date not recorded
  Reliability CI harness for browser agents that runs identical web tasks across mutating DOM conditions to measure resilience.
- [agentic-browser-boundary-tests](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-larrypeseckis-agentic-browser-boundary-tests/) — Date not recorded
  Local test suite of prompt-injection and instruction-authority boundary scenarios with canary checks for AI browsers and web agents.
- [AgentReady](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-williehems-agentready/) — Date not recorded
  Cloud-browser evaluation harness that tests whether an autonomous AI agent can complete a website's primary conversion action.
- [AgentRewardBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-mcgill-nlp-agent-reward-bench/) — Date not recorded
  A benchmark and evaluation toolkit for comparing automatic web-agent judges against expert judgments of task success, side effects and repetition.
- [AssistantBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-oriyor-assistantbench/) — Date not recorded
  AssistantBench evaluates web agents on realistic, time-consuming tasks. Its project repository links to the task dataset and submission leaderboard.
- [Bananalyzer](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-reworkd-bananalyzer/) — Date not recorded
  Bananalyzer is an evaluation framework and dataset suite for testing web agents on structured data extraction and navigation tasks.
- [BearCubs](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-86229ebd22143128bcf7/) — Date not recorded
  A live-web benchmark with 111 questions testing agents' ability to browse and extract factual answers through text and multimodal interactions.
- [BrowseComp](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/component-openai-simple-evals-browsecomp/) — Date not recorded
  OpenAI's 1,266-question benchmark for browsing agents, whose short, verifiable answers require persistent search and navigation across many websites and are graded by a model judge.
- [Browser Agent Comparison (4 Approaches)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-ismael-joffroy-chandoutis-browser-agent-comparison/) — Date not recorded
  Compares four AI browser-control paradigms (Chrome extension, Playwright, Browser Use V3, and CLI) on task completion and cost.
- [Browser Automation Benchmark (Python vs Rust)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-tashonbraganca-browser-automation-benchmark/) — Date not recorded
  Benchmarks Python Playwright, Rust playwright-rs, and chromiumoxide on headless browser startup, navigation, and screenshot latency.
- [Browser LLM Perf](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-dtopuzov-browser-llm-perf/) — Date not recorded
  Browser LLM Perf compares browser automation CLIs (CraftDriver vs Playwright CLI) on task completion speed and LLM token cost.
- [Browser Use Benchmark (BU Bench)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-browser-use-benchmark/) — Date not recorded
  Browser Use Benchmark (BU Bench V2.1) evaluates browser agents across 200 real-world web navigation and interaction tasks.
- [browser-eval](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-lexmount-browser-eval/) — Date not recorded
  browser-eval is an evaluation harness for web fetching, deterministic browser trajectory replay, and autonomous web agent benchmarking.
- [browser-task-bench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-tradestackdev-browser-task-bench/) — Date not recorded
  browser-task-bench is a benchmark suite of 200+ web interaction tasks covering forms, search, authentication, and checkout flows.
- [BrowserAgentRegression](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-albertxxuu-browseragentregression/) — Date not recorded
  Local-first regression testing benchmark for evaluating browser agents against controlled UI and DOM mutations.
- [BrowserART](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-scaleapi-browser-art/) — Date not recorded
  Red-teaming benchmark and test suite from Scale AI evaluating whether refusal-trained language models can be jailbroken when deployed as browser agents.
- [BrowserTransactionBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-lamenting-hawthorn-browserbench/) — Date not recorded
  BrowserTransactionBench evaluates transactional safety, idempotency, and double-spend prevention in autonomous browser agents.
- [BrowseWebApp Bench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-visnia-ai-browsewebapp-bench/) — Date not recorded
  BrowseWebApp Bench evaluates browser agents across 100 realistic web workflows spanning record extraction, document upload, and form submission.
- [CAPTCHA Gym](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-mildchef-muse-captcha-gym/) — Date not recorded
  CAPTCHA Gym is a self-hosted evaluation platform for browser agents featuring controlled reCAPTCHA-style visual challenges and server scoring.
- [CaptchaArena](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-x0x0x00-captchaarena/) — Date not recorded
  CaptchaArena provides a live server and fine-grained trajectory dataset across 20 interactive CAPTCHA types for evaluating computer-use agents.
- [ClawBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-tiger-ai-lab-clawbench/) — Date not recorded
  Everyday-task benchmark on live websites that runs browser agents in containerized Chromium, blocks each final submission request for scoring by URL match and an LLM judge, and records five layers of run evidence.
- [CodexQA](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-openqa-cn-codexqa/) — Date not recorded
  Suite of 11 local-first QA Agent Skills for Cursor, Claude Code, and Codex to verify live web and desktop user journeys.
- [Collision Canary](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-mystiquemide-collision-canary/) — Date not recorded
  Multi-actor invariant verification harness that drives two concurrent browser agents against a shared backend to uncover race bugs.
- [cskwork vigil](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-cskwork-vigil/) — Date not recorded
  Continuous browser QA harness that reproduces reported bugs and autonomously expands end-to-end test coverage on live web URLs.
- [CUA Eval Kit](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-marwanalsiddiq-cua-eval-kit/) — Date not recorded
  Controlled browser fixture and 10-task computer-use evaluation suite for grading web agent accuracy and step efficiency.
- [CUAVerifierBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-d5140d438466c1e1348a/) — Date not recorded
  Human-annotated browser-agent trajectories for comparing automated task-success and process-quality verifiers against reviewer judgments.
- [decision-luna-browse-control](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-humanity-forever-decision-luna-browse-control/) — Date not recorded
  Evaluates typed System-One decision models (Luna Decisions and Jev) driving real Chromium browser workflows on synthetic web apps.
- [deedchain](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-pillcrew-deedchain/) — Date not recorded
  deedchain benchmarks report fidelity and hallucination rates of browser agents against recorded ground-truth web execution traces.
- [dom-drift-bench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-notel1gible-dom-drift-bench/) — Date not recorded
  dom-drift-bench evaluates four web extraction and grounding strategies across ten categories of structural DOM drift.
- [drift-audit](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-sebastianpuchet-drift-audit/) — Date not recorded
  Audits and classifies a browser-automation repository's selector maintenance history to quantify UI drift fragility.
- [DSH QA](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-zseven-w-dsh-qa/) — Date not recorded
  DeepSeek Harness QA plugin where an autonomous browser agent explores live web applications to discover functional and UX bugs.
- [Effectprint](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-tommytranx-effectprint/) — Date not recorded
  Deterministic behavioral contract auditing suite for verifying side effects of WebMCP browser-agent tools.
- [Ego Browser Benchmark Framework](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-citrolabs-ego-browser-benchmark-framework/) — Date not recorded
  Ego Browser Benchmark Framework is a reproducible harness for comparing foundation models on controlled browser-use agent tasks.
- [EIA](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-osu-nlp-group-eia-against-webagent/) — Date not recorded
  Evaluation suite for Environmental Injection Attacks that embed invisible malicious HTML elements into benign websites to test privacy leakage in generalist web agents.
- [ExactDelta (ActionProof)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-tsuchiyatakahirolab-actionproof/) — Date not recorded
  Post-action state-delta verification harness for WebMCP browser agents that detects silent UI and state failures.
- [FixtureBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-prabal-singh-fixturebench/) — Date not recorded
  FixtureBench evaluates procurement browser agents on deterministic Coupa-style buyer/supplier portals with pagination, MFA, and decoy POs.
- [Graph-Guided Agentic Testing](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-avikundal-graph-guided-agentic-testing/) — Date not recorded
  Uses a browser agent to explore web checkout flows into a Neo4j state graph and synthesizes graph-guided end-to-end tests.
- [HarnessBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-reacher-z-harnessbench/) — Date not recorded
  Benchmark harness that holds the base model fixed and runs six browser-agent harnesses in per-harness containers on ClawBench's everyday online tasks, reusing ClawBench's recording and judging pipeline.
- [HumanQA](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-hotragn-humanqa/) — Date not recorded
  Evidence-first product quality and UX evaluation harness for testing web applications with browser agents.
- [Intentproof](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-egagofur-intentproof/) — Date not recorded
  AI-driven end-to-end verification orchestrator and interactive browser testing harness.
- [Kane](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-venkat5599-kane/) — Date not recorded
  Plain-English browser verification harness for AI-built web applications that reports UI failures back to coding agents.
- [Kernel Eval Protocol](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-kernel-kernel-eval-protocol-quickstart/) — Date not recorded
  Evaluates and fine-tunes vision-language browser agents using Eval Protocol, Kernel cloud browsers, and verifiable task graders.
- [KNOWS Benchmark](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-alexgill321-knows-benchmark/) — Date not recorded
  KNOWS benchmarks web agents on realistic, open-ended Google Workspace tasks spanning Google Docs, Sheets, and Slides.
- [LAST DOOR](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-agentsimdev-last-door/) — Date not recorded
  Authentication resilience benchmark and WebMCP authority compiler for testing browser agents at login and permission boundaries.
- [Lexbench-Headless-Browser](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-lexmount-lexbench-headless-browser/) — Date not recorded
  Lexbench-Headless-Browser benchmarks 13 headless browser automation runtimes across 1,928 web tasks.
- [LLM as a Judge for Computer-Use Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-anaishowland-llm-judge-psai/) — Date not recorded
  LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation harness for assessing computer-use agent trajectories across web browsing and desktop interaction tasks.
- [MalURLBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-jiangyinger-malurlbench/) — Date not recorded
  MalURLBench evaluates the vulnerability of LLM-based web agents to phishing and malicious URL attacks across ten real-world web scenarios.
- [MarketBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-santos-sanz-marketbench/) — Date not recorded
  MarketBench is a reproducible WebMCP benchmark evaluating browser agents on multi-week economic decision-making in simulated markets.
- [mimiq](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-gojiplus-mimiq/) — Date not recorded
  mimiq is a dynamic end-to-end agentic simulation and web UI evaluation runner integrating Cypress, Playwright, and Stagehand.
- [Mind2Web](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-osu-nlp-group-mind2web/) — Date not recorded
  Human demonstrations and tasks for generalist web navigation research.
- [Mind2Web 2](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-osu-nlp-group-mind2web-2/) — Date not recorded
  Long-horizon agentic web search benchmark whose Agent-as-a-Judge framework scores time-varying, citation-backed answers, with evaluation scripts for its dev and test sets.
- [Mirage (Adversarial Web Benchmark)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-shushant17711-mirage/) — Date not recorded
  Controlled adversarial web environment for measuring how autonomous browser agents fail under deceptive UI and prompt traps.
- [MMSearch-Plus](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-mmsearch-plus-mmsearch-plus/) — Date not recorded
  A benchmark of 311 visual reasoning tasks for multimodal browsing agents, requiring iterative image and text retrieval. The repository links the dataset and provides a loading helper.
- [Navi-Bench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-yutori-ai-navi-bench/) — Date not recorded
  Navi-Bench evaluates web agents on everyday consumer tasks executed directly on live websites with deterministic outcome verifiers.
- [Next-Gen CAPTCHAs](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-metaagentx-nextgen-captchas/) — Date not recorded
  Next-Gen CAPTCHAs provides an interactive benchmark and generator of cognitive-gap CAPTCHA challenges for defending against GUI web agents.
- [Nightshift](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-yayaq1-nightshift/) — Date not recorded
  Black-box computer-use QA harness on Solari that evaluates live user interfaces without access to application test hooks.
- [Odysseys](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-ljang0-odysseys/) — Date not recorded
  200 long-horizon web tasks drawn from real browsing sessions, run on the live Internet in Chrome through the OSWorld runner and scored per rubric item by an LLM judge over the trajectory's screenshots and actions.
- [OmniWebBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-fanxutherealone-omniwebbench/) — Date not recorded
  OmniWebBench evaluates agentic browsers across end-to-end web navigation, action execution, failure recovery, and evidence verification.
- [Online Mind2Web](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-osu-nlp-group-online-mind2web/) — Date not recorded
  300 tasks on 136 live websites for evaluating web agents online, with outdated tasks replaced over time, human-reviewed submissions and the WebJudge automatic evaluator.
- [only-cli/benchmarks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-only-cli-benchmarks/) — Date not recorded
  A reproducible benchmark suite compares tools that agents use to read live web pages, measuring content retrieval, time and token use across page views and browsing tasks.
- [Open Operator Evals](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-nottelabs-open-operator-evals/) — Date not recorded
  Archived Notte Labs harness and results that run web agents eight times on a 30-task WebVoyager subset, judging each run with a GPT-4o screenshot judge and comparing it with the agent's self-reported success.
- [Open User](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-tiacoo-open-user/) — Date not recorded
  Black-box UX audit harness that dispatches AI browser personas to test web products and record friction points.
- [pantheon-webarena](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-pantheon-auto-pantheon-webarena/) — Date not recorded
  pantheon-webarena packages containerized WebArena environments with reset, health-check, and parallel evaluation utilities for web agents.
- [Pathproof](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-mikulabc-pathproof/) — Date not recorded
  Local-first AI-assisted website regression discovery suite with deterministic Playwright replay.
- [Persona UX Test](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-ericwong5021-persona-ux-test/) — Date not recorded
  Blind-tests web and desktop user interfaces using isolated AI personas that interact strictly through rendered screens.
- [Plasmate Benchmarks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-plasmate-labs-plasmate-benchmarks/) — Date not recorded
  Plasmate Benchmarks provides reproducible latency, memory, and token-efficiency evaluations for the Plasmate semantic browser engine.
- [pms-race](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-lgoyal6-pms-race/) — Date not recorded
  Concurrency benchmark for computer-use agents operating a legacy property-management web UI to measure race conditions and double-bookings.
- [PoisonPage](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-redbotster-poisonpage/) — Date not recorded
  Adversarial testbed demonstrating indirect prompt injection and API-key exfiltration against AI shopping web agents.
- [REAL](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-agi-inc-real/) — Date not recorded
  A browser-agent evaluation toolkit with reproducible website replicas, task goals, an agent API, and a benchmarking harness.
- [Resurf](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-lightfeed-resurf/) — Date not recorded
  Resurf is a deterministic, reproducible test environment for evaluating AI browser agents across realistic web failure modes.
- [Simulation Labs](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-satyamdave-simulation-labs/) — Date not recorded
  Behavioral CI gate where computer-use agents test signup, checkout, and onboarding flows on every deployment.
- [solari-playtest](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-moazessam376-dev-solari-playtest/) — Date not recorded
  Autonomous playtesting agent and evaluation harness on Solari that builds web games from Git and tests gameplay in a real browser.
- [Spider Browser Dataset](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-spider-rs-spider-browser-dataset/) — Date not recorded
  Spider Browser Dataset benchmarks browser automation and anti-bot resilience across 999 URLs and 327 domains in 18 categories.
- [ST-WebAgentBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-segev-shlomov-st-webagentbench/) — Date not recorded
  BrowserGym-based benchmark for evaluating the safety and policy compliance of enterprise web agents across six trustworthiness risk dimensions.
- [taskproof](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-taskproof-taskproof/) — Date not recorded
  taskproof is an open-source CI harness that tests whether real AI computer-use and browser agents can complete tasks on live websites.
- [TestPilot](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-h0ngza1-testpilot/) — Date not recorded
  Agentic end-to-end web testing platform that executes natural-language test cases in live browsers.
- [TimeWarp](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-sparklabutah-timewarp/) — Date not recorded
  Web-agent benchmark on self-hosted Wiki, News and Shop sites, each with six era-styled UI versions, registering 231 BrowserGym tasks scored mainly by deterministic answer verifiers.
- [TurkingBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-808a22468ee0bf565d6e/) — Date not recorded
  A web-agent benchmark built from crowdsourcing HTML tasks, with 32.2K instances across 158 tasks and a framework that applies model responses to page controls.
- [UserZero](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-aafre-userzero/) — Date not recorded
  Synthetic first-user evaluation harness that verifies web applications by attempting onboarding and core flows with AI browser agents.
- [VideoWebArena](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-ljang0-videowebarena/) — Date not recorded
  VideoWebArena evaluates web agents that use video tutorials to complete website tasks. Its released benchmark includes task configurations, environments, and an evaluation pipeline.
- [WAInjectBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-norrrrrrr-lyn-wainjectbench/) — Date not recorded
  WAInjectBench benchmarks prompt injection detection in web agents across six text and image attack types targeting web interaction pipelines.
- [WATCHER](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-aaman-911-watcher/) — Date not recorded
  Prompt-injection defense classifier, evaluation benchmark, and defended browser harness for autonomous web agents.
- [Web Agent Comparison](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-pleasedodisturb-web-agent-comparison/) — Date not recorded
  Reproducible 8-dimension benchmark comparing 7 browser-automation MCP servers on latency, token overhead, and task success.
- [web-agent-bench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-nsosio-web-agent-bench/) — Date not recorded
  web-agent-bench compares browser-automation harnesses (Playwright MCP, agent-browser, Stagehand) on token usage, latency, and task completion.
- [web-agent-eval (REAL Benchmark Runner)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-saarthak-yadav-web-agent-eval/) — Date not recorded
  Evaluates web agents on 102 replica websites from the REAL benchmark under explicit step, token, and budget constraints.
- [WebArena](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-web-arena-x-webarena/) — Date not recorded
  Self-hosted web environments and tasks for evaluating autonomous web agents.
- [WebArena-Infinity](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-web-arena-x-webarena-infinity/) — Date not recorded
  Generated web apps modeled on GitLab, PayPal, Xero, Elation EHR and other products, each with resettable state and Python-verified tasks for browser agents, plus the coding-agent pipeline that builds them.
- [WebArena-Verified](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-servicenow-webarena-verified/) — Date not recorded
  An audited re-release of WebArena's 812 web tasks with corrected reference answers and deterministic evaluators that score structured agent responses and captured network traces, plus a 258-task hard subset.
- [WebBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-halluminate-webbench/) — Date not recorded
  Live-web benchmark from Halluminate and Skyvern with read, create, update, delete and file-download tasks on 452 high-traffic websites, including logins and form entry, with released per-agent run results.
- [WebClick](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-5e2bd1b3398974b79c9c/) — Date not recorded
  A web-interface localization benchmark with screenshot instructions and target boxes, including agent browsing, human browsing and calendar cases.
- [WebGen-Bench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-mnluzimu-webgen-bench/) — Date not recorded
  WebGen-Bench evaluates generated websites with automated tests, including a UI agent that carries out interactions and checks outcomes. The repository supplies evaluation code and data.
- [WebGuard](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-osu-nlp-group-webguard/) — Date not recorded
  Codebase, annotation toolkit, and action-level risk dataset for training and evaluating guardrail models that flag unsafe browser-agent actions before execution.
- [WebLINX](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-mcgill-nlp-weblinx/) — Date not recorded
  Demonstrations and evaluation for conversational web navigation.
- [WebLINX 1.1 (WebLINX-BrowserGym)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-77afb3a9b045a157167c/) — Date not recorded
  WebLINX 1.1, a reprocessed release of WebLINX's conversational web-navigation demonstrations packaged as single-step BrowserGym tasks that score one predicted browser action against the recorded turn.
- [WebMall](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-wbsg-uni-mannheim-webmall/) — Date not recorded
  WebMall is a multi-shop e-commerce web agent benchmark evaluating cross-site price comparison, product search, and cart checkout.
- [WebMCP Description Benchmarks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-clouatre-labs-webmcp-description-benchmarks/) — Date not recorded
  WebMCP Description Benchmarks evaluates how in-browser WebMCP tool schema descriptions affect agent tool selection and execution accuracy.
- [WebReplayBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-mahirlabibdihan-webreplaybench/) — Date not recorded
  A web agent benchmark with a local shop site, BrowserGym tasks, and database audit records for measuring task completion and repeated writes during backtracking.
- [WebRetriever](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-mininglamp-ai-webretriever/) — Date not recorded
  WebRetriever supplies a web-agent evaluation runner and a judge that assesses task completion from captured requests, actions and page URLs.
- [WebRewardBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-ac6d0f80765f5a32255a/) — Date not recorded
  An offline preference-evaluation set for web process reward models, pairing page and trajectory context with preferred and rejected candidate decisions.
- [WebShop](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-princeton-nlp-webshop/) — Date not recorded
  Simulated shopping environment for instruction-following web agents.
- [WebsiteBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-tiger-ai-lab-websitebench/) — Date not recorded
  Website reconstruction benchmark: agents explore a production-level site through a browser, rebuild a full-stack version, and are scored by hidden functional and visual test cases across 70 tasks.
- [WebTailBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-3b6dfd52f98bfe76bc7f/) — Date not recorded
  Live-web task definitions and rubrics for evaluating computer-use agents, with preserved original tasks, a refreshed V2 task suite and a separate refusal set.
- [WebTestBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-friedrichor-webtestbench/) — Date not recorded
  An end-to-end web-testing benchmark where agents derive test checklists from development instructions, interact with deployed applications and report functional or constraint-related defects.
- [WebUIProof](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-yunyuntsai-webuiproof-benchmark/) — Date not recorded
  A benchmark of 149 web UI generation tasks with structured test cases and a vision-language agent that tests generated interfaces in a browser.
- [WebVoyager](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-minorjerry-webvoyager/) — Date not recorded
  Multimodal web navigation agent and evaluation tasks on real websites.
- [WebWalker](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-alibaba-nlp-webwalker/) — Date not recorded
  Benchmark and multi-agent framework for evaluating large language models on multi-step web traversal and deep information-seeking tasks.
- [Wornpage WebMCP Conformance](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-wornpage-webmcp-conformance/) — Date not recorded
  Validation toolkit checking page-owned WebMCP tool schemas, authorization boundaries, and execution conformance.

## 数据集

- [AgentRewardBench dataset](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-866251b68d6bba8b8ba6/) — Date not recorded
  Hugging Face dataset release for AgentRewardBench containing trajectory evaluation instances across WebArena, VisualWebArena, AssistantBench, WorkArena, and OSWorld.
- [BrowserART](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-dec73112f4c2dad889cb/) — Date not recorded
  A browser-agent safety dataset of 100 harmful browser-related behaviors, designed for red-team evaluation across real and synthetic websites.
- [Cie1/MMSearch-Plus](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-8fd4e25ae5c0fe619969/) — Date not recorded
  A dataset of 311 image-based tasks for evaluating multimodal browsing agents that use iterative image and text retrieval to answer questions.
- [ClawBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-9c96a767b56ba0eb4494/) — Date not recorded
  A browser-agent task corpus for everyday work on live websites, with V1 and V2 test splits, task context, and schemas that intercept final submissions.
- [Go-Browse-WA processed data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-a13a187bc058fbb4f6af/) — Date not recorded
  Flattened web-interaction training records with accessibility-tree observations, assistant completions, trajectory rewards and step-link metadata.
- [go-browse-wa-raw](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-edb0eadcc3df5559e941/) — Date not recorded
  go-browse-wa-raw is a browser-agent trajectory dataset with page observations, actions, goals, rewards, and images. Its train split contains 196462 examples.
- [GUI-Libra-81K-SFT](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-f6e1a7527f9346ed1c1d/) — Date not recorded
  SFT release of GUI-Libra-81K: per-step reasoning traces and coordinate-grounded actions over web and Android screenshots, in JSON annotation files with full, no-reasoning and 1,000-sample variants.
- [InSTA 150k Tasks (v3)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-a80c7330dbfd4f897693/) — Date not recorded
  About 150k web navigation tasks from the InSTA project, each giving a website domain, an instruction, suggested steps and success criteria, with train and test splits drawn from different websites.
- [LexBench-Trajectory-Replay](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-c049eba816b97d5076d0/) — Date not recorded
  LexBench-Trajectory-Replay provides 30 public-website tasks with fixed browser actions and assertions for evaluating browser execution and retrieval.
- [LexBench-WebArena-Replay](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-a96c70bcf5a235f980b6/) — Date not recorded
  LexBench-WebArena-Replay pairs WebArena task definitions with fixed browser actions so Browser Eval can replay them and score completion across a published 812-task set.
- [lumos_unified_ground_iterative](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-87bba046fba8c1cd968c/) — Date not recorded
  A JSONL training dataset for the Lumos-I grounding module, combining math, question-answering, and web-agent annotations, including examples from Mind2Web.
- [lumos_unified_plan_iterative](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-543aff764901777e6ad5/) — Date not recorded
  lumos_unified_plan_iterative is a JSONL training dataset for the Lumos planning module. It combines web-agent examples with math and complex question-answering tasks.
- [lumos_web_agent_plan_iterative](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-0772193ae2967c407d22/) — Date not recorded
  A Lumos-Iterative dataset for training a web agent's planning module, with 1009 Mind2Web task annotations in the described data overview.
- [Magma-Mind2Web-SoM](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-e8256e338a9c379ca85b/) — Date not recorded
  Magma-Mind2Web-SoM provides Mind2Web data for downstream fine-tuning of the Magma multimodal agent model.
- [McGill-NLP/safearena](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-be4ca14d1724b0cf8314/) — Date not recorded
  SafeArena supplies safe and harmful task splits for evaluating the safety of autonomous web agents, with accompanying environment archives under gated access.
- [Mind2Web 2 task data and judge scripts](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-4f4c4f0bf0cec93c59d1/) — Date not recorded
  Auto-gated Hub release of Mind2Web 2: dev and test task CSVs for long-horizon agentic web search, plus 130 per-task Python evaluation scripts that act as its judge agents.
- [Mind2Web bbox eval (MultiUI)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-56b5936190d20fa3d1c1/) — Date not recorded
  Mind2Web steps repackaged by the MultiUI authors as JPEG screenshots with per-step JSONL queries and candidate-element boxes, for MultiUI's screenshot-based Mind2Web action evaluation.
- [Mind2Web data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-380a6eedcb4de7778e88/) — Date not recorded
  Hub release of Mind2Web's crowdsourced web-task demonstrations: train-split JSON with raw and cleaned HTML, candidate elements and CLICK/TYPE/SELECT steps, plus a password-protected archive of the three test splits.
- [Mind2Web-Live](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-03ffa3f28413bc2edb1b/) — Date not recorded
  Task files for the WebCanvas live-web benchmark: 438 training and 104 test tasks derived from Mind2Web, each with key-node checks on URLs or page elements that score intermediate progress.
- [MT-Mind2Web](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-bdfb50330deb97893ce4/) — Date not recorded
  Multi-turn web-navigation conversations constructed from Mind2Web interactions, with dialogue turns, HTML snapshots, element candidates and click/type/select action sequences.
- [Multimodal-Mind2Web](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-66f780b493156dc7efe5/) — Date not recorded
  Mind2Web action steps aligned with the matching webpage screenshot, plus raw and cleaned HTML, candidate elements and the target operation, in a train split and test splits held out by task, website and domain.
- [NAIL-Group/ClawBench](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-146edbaee6701bdb6ee0/) — Date not recorded
  NAIL-Group/ClawBench releases task data for evaluating browser agents on live websites. Its card describes V1 and V2 corpora, task metadata, and loading through the datasets library.
- [NAIL-Group/ClawBenchV1Trace](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-e7653b062bded670198a/) — Date not recorded
  ClawBench V1 Traces releases browser-agent run records with screen video, actions, network traffic and agent messages for replay, debugging and regrading.
- [NNetNav Live data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-4f47abde8cd28a1ec1e1/) — Date not recorded
  Browser-interaction data released with NNetNav for its live-website setting, with separate training and test files.
- [NNetNav WebArena data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-25a4d0ab29b60250863a/) — Date not recorded
  NNetNav's WebArena-oriented browser-interaction data release, with separate training and test files.
- [Online-Mind2Web task data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-a252b36bf1c440269a2e/) — Date not recorded
  Gated Hub release of the Online-Mind2Web task set: one JSON test split of tasks on live websites, 300 tasks on 136 sites per the project README, which the publishers revise when sites change.
- [OpenJev-Data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-a54a35c90681ced60cec/) — Date not recorded
  OpenJev-Data provides labeled browser steps for training models to choose a web agent's next operation and target element, plus a fixed test set for endpoint evaluation.
- [OS-Genesis web training data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-31768421b4ecd1ad89f7/) — Date not recorded
  Gated OS-Genesis web release holding one JSONL file of reverse-task-synthesis training trajectories and a screenshot archive, named in the project README as training data for its WA checkpoints.
- [pae-data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-72301bf2dec104205c8d/) — Date not recorded
  pae-data releases data associated with PAE research on internet agents, including task files for WebArena and WebVoyager and model checkpoint files.
- [Plan-and-Act training data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-ba6453cfdae0eb049ced/) — Date not recorded
