  Planner and action-executor training examples for WebArena-Lite and WebVoyager, with model-specific processed files and raw trajectory annotations.
- [TimeWarp](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-8be414a28d1984abb1b9/) — Date not recorded
  Dataset of 231 web-agent goals for Wiki, News, and Shop sites across six interface eras, with train and test splits and evaluator specifications.
- [VeriGUI](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-8477ada5b7de1d399c9d/) — Date not recorded
  VeriWeb dataset of 302 web information-seeking tasks, with annotated subtasks, human action logs, and execution videos for evaluating long-chain agents.
- [VisualWebBench dataset](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-149769d8bf07b7fbabdc/) — Date not recorded
  Hugging Face dataset release for VisualWebBench spanning seven visual web understanding, OCR, element grounding, and action prediction tasks.
- [WebArena-Infinity browser agent trajectories](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-ccac8d260d6d1d330bf5/) — Date not recorded
  Passing runs of three browser agents on 13 WebArena-Infinity web apps generated from product documentation, with per-step reasoning, actions, screenshots and programmatic verifier results.
- [WebChain (original release)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-116431f8ea32dbc6d637/) — Date not recorded
  Original WebChain release of human-annotated trajectories on live websites, with raw JSON records, viewport and partial full-page screenshots, and Parquet files for high-level planning and low-level grounding steps.
- [WebChain v2](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-56f08987cc19b82dc607/) — Date not recorded
  Cleaned v2 release of WebChain's human web trajectories, packaged as trajectory-level and Seed-style sliding-window SFT Parquet with aligned screenshots, plus action metadata and a 150-trace evaluation subset.
- [WebEvolver SFT data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-e126bbd9729ebdaf05ab/) — Date not recorded
  Two JSONL collections for supervised fine-tuning the WebEvolver agent policy and web world model.
- [WebGuard](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-df10c80a8da55773154d/) — Date not recorded
  Dataset of 5,999 browser-action safety annotations, pairing website and action details with risk classifications for web-agent research.
- [WebLINX dataset](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-1517e989c470563bd08d/) — Date not recorded
  Hub data of WebLINX 1.0: conversational web-navigation demonstrations preprocessed into per-turn records with dialogue, cleaned HTML, candidate elements and action history, plus reranking data.
- [WebPRM checklist-generation data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-fcc98b149272bbf31b05/) — Date not recorded
  Web-task context, starting observations and action history paired with structured checklists and subgoal analyses for checklist generation.
- [WebPRM Collection preference data](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-8dfc84eb4016204fe70a/) — Date not recorded
  Checklist-annotated web decision records with page observations, prior actions, one preferred continuation and rejected alternatives for process-reward learning.
- [WebSRC](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-x-lance-websrc-baseline/) — Date not recorded
  WebSRC provides baseline models and processing pipelines for the Web-Based Structural Reading Comprehension dataset over HTML and screenshots.

## 模型

- [ACT-1](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-f885d17601171253d25e/) — Date not recorded
  Adept's 2022 action transformer, previewed in demo videos of a Chrome extension that reads a custom rendering of the page and clicks, types and scrolls on its UI elements to carry out typed requests.
- [AgentTrek-1.0-32B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-c3ab0f006a880174e645/) — Date not recorded
  A web-agent checkpoint fine-tuned from Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct using the AgentTrek trajectory-synthesis project. The release includes model weights.
- [BU-30B-A3B-Preview](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-2c4b53bc04f33578e9b8/) — Date not recorded
  Browser Use's open-weight browser-agent model, fine-tuned from Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B-Instruct (30B total, 3B active parameters) to serve as the LLM of the browser-use library through BU Cloud or vLLM.
- [Claude Sonnet 4.5](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-18816edf464a073a0120/) — Date not recorded
  Claude Sonnet 4.5 is a general model available through an API. Anthropic reports its computer-use performance on OSWorld and demonstrates browser tasks.
- [Dreamer-7B-Classifieds](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-b7ccf7acb2e9d8a11853/) — Date not recorded
  Dreamer-7B-Classifieds is a WebDreamer checkpoint for the Classifieds web domain. It supports planning by simulating the results of candidate actions.
- [Dreamer-7B-Reddit](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-dfa8047521d1f3b3aa54/) — Date not recorded
  Dreamer-7B-Reddit is a Reddit-domain checkpoint for WebDreamer. It predicts the screen state after a proposed action to support web-agent planning.
- [Dreamer-7B-Shopping](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-3173abbb295c74d0e9ec/) — Date not recorded
  A shopping-domain Dreamer-7B checkpoint for WebDreamer, which uses screenshot-conditioned predictions of candidate web actions to support agent planning.
- [espetro/htlm-lfm2.5-350m](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-df38112df002157ce4b0/) — Date not recorded
  A fine-tuned LFM2.5-350M model that selects click, type, or select actions from an instruction and indexed web elements, with a GGUF file for browser inference.
- [Fara](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-microsoft-fara/) — Date not recorded
  Microsoft browser-use agent models that act from screenshots by predicting click coordinates without accessibility trees; Fara1.5 comes in 4B, 9B and 27B, succeeding Fara-7B.
- [Fara 7B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-a4da77ee63f879cc8e63/) — Date not recorded
  Microsoft's Fara checkpoint, a Qwen2.5-VL-architecture image-text-to-text model with about 8.3B BF16 parameters, English, MIT-licensed and linked to arXiv paper 2511.19663.
- [Gemini 2.5 Computer Use](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-44827a16f49e65ba8fb3/) — Date not recorded
  Preview Google DeepMind model built on Gemini 2.5 Pro and post-trained for UI control, returning browser and mobile actions from screenshots and recent action history through the Gemini API computer_use tool.
- [Go-Browse WebArena Qwen 7B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-db1b7b7efe3755ae5693/) — Date not recorded
  A Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct checkpoint fine-tuned on Go-Browse WebArena trajectories for website task execution.
- [GUI-Libra 3B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-ae8e3fa5a67a608804f8/) — Date not recorded
  GUI-Libra checkpoint fine-tuned from Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct (about 4.07B BF16 parameters) that turns a screenshot, task and action history into a <think> reasoning block and a JSON action with point_2d coordinates.
- [GUI-Libra 4B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-9082bb5effe5d1a1cada/) — Date not recorded
  GUI-Libra checkpoint fine-tuned from Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct (about 4.83B BF16 parameters) that turns a screenshot, task and action history into a <thinking> reasoning block and a JSON action with point_2d coordinates.
- [GUI-Libra 7B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-f52794224f9541fd9eb3/) — Date not recorded
  GUI-Libra checkpoint fine-tuned from Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct (about 8.29B BF16 parameters) that turns a screenshot, task and action history into a <think> reasoning block and a JSON action with point_2d coordinates.
- [GUI-Libra 8B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-6694391b0cf50389d2e9/) — Date not recorded
  GUI-Libra checkpoint fine-tuned from Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct (about 8.77B BF16 parameters) that turns a screenshot, task and action history into a <thinking> reasoning block and a JSON action with point_2d coordinates.
- [Holo-3.1-35B-A3B-FP8](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-566bd1469811983951f3/) — Date not recorded
  An FP8 checkpoint in the Holo3.1 vision-language model family for computer-use agents. The card describes web, desktop and mobile tasks and links to deployment guidance.
- [Holo-3.1-35B-A3B-NVFP4](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-aeffabf23c16f8cf2139/) — Date not recorded
  Holo-3.1-35B-A3B-NVFP4 is a quantized computer-use vision-language checkpoint for web, desktop and mobile tasks, with function-calling support.
- [Holo1 3B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-c491c446a71cac1def66/) — Date not recorded
  The 3B Holo1 action VLM, fine-tuned from Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct to act as policy, UI localizer or answer validator inside the Surfer-H browser agent.
- [Holo1 7B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-f35cfcbaeb79663eabeb/) — Date not recorded
  A Qwen2.5-VL-based action model released for the Surfer-H web-agent system, supporting policy, interface-localization and validation roles.
- [Holo4-27B-NVFP4](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-b1457a20d437732b60f7/) — Date not recorded
  A quantized Holo4 vision-language checkpoint for computer use across web, desktop, and mobile interfaces, with a harness that executes requested GUI and tool actions.
- [Holotron-12B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-6c16f91d8d79d7836283/) — Date not recorded
  Computer-use policy checkpoint from H Company and NVIDIA, fine-tuned from Nemotron Nano 12B v2 VL on localization and navigation data, with a hybrid SSM-attention backbone for high-throughput multi-image inference.
- [HTLM](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-espetro-htlm/) — Date not recorded
  HTLM is a compact 350M browser-agent model fine-tuned on Mind2Web for fast on-device and in-browser DOM action prediction.
- [kev-0.6b-browser-use](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-3c55f225a1f66a6261ac/) — Date not recorded
  kev-0.6b-browser-use is a 0.6B browser decision model that scores candidate page elements and chooses a click, type, or select operation from a task goal.
- [kev-0.6b-browser-use-ONNX](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-295e0be27996e41738f8/) — Date not recorded
  A WebGPU-compatible ONNX browser-use model that selects a page element and operation from a goal and candidate elements, with a 4-bit export for JavaScript use.
- [kev-browser-use](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-arbazsiddiqui-kev-browser-use/) — Date not recorded
  kev-browser-use is a compact 0.6B System-One action model fine-tuned on Mind2Web for low-latency browser element selection.
- [laya-browser](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-c5b0e2eab771f62792fb/) — Date not recorded
  Non-generative browser-agent policy fine-tuned from the 322M laya encoder: one forward pass over page text and the interactive-element list picks the next operation and its target element.
- [Llama-3-8B-Web](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-7c3e8c4ba52428584079/) — Date not recorded
  A Llama 3 8B instruction-model checkpoint fine-tuned on WebLINX web interactions and dialogue, predicting browser actions from a textual state representation.
- [Llama8b-NNetNav-WA](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-c9d2a9288a568db12bd1/) — Date not recorded
  A Llama checkpoint trained on demonstrations gathered through WebArena exploration. It takes a task instruction and selects browser actions to navigate webpages.
- [lumos_unified_ground_iterative-13B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-b0b2cd616f8c30b4b09d/) — Date not recorded
  A released Lumos grounding-module checkpoint fine-tuned on web-agent tasks alongside question answering, multimodal and math tasks.
- [lumos_web_agent_ground_iterative](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-246241a0b6219513cb7f/) — Date not recorded
  A released Lumos grounding checkpoint fine-tuned for web-agent tasks using the Lumos-Iterative formulation. It serves the grounding module of a modular agent.
- [lumos_web_agent_ground_iterative-13B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-dca5b0d8449331bbede4/) — Date not recorded
  A released Lumos checkpoint for grounding web-agent actions within an iterative architecture that separates planning, grounding and execution.
- [lumos_web_agent_plan_iterative](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-e35db30fee209ef9b57d/) — Date not recorded
  A fine-tuned Lumos planning-module checkpoint for web-agent tasks, released as model weights alongside a separately listed training dataset.
- [lumos_web_agent_plan_iterative-13B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-7e7de336b1d8a2adce2c/) — Date not recorded
  A released Lumos checkpoint for planning web-agent tasks within an iterative architecture that separates planning, grounding and execution.
- [OpAgent-32B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-875b417b3ef43021cd74/) — Date not recorded
  A vision-language model fine-tuned for web navigation. It takes a task instruction and webpage screenshot and produces a browser action or final answer.
- [OpenCSG-Qwen2.5-7B-GUI](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-2462fb9d529bac7fa896/) — Date not recorded
  A browser automation model for the AutoHub plugin that accepts natural-language requests for web operations and information retrieval.
- [OpenJev](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-alanhuangyoo-openjev/) — Date not recorded
  Decision models at 1.7B, 4B and 8B that answer typed questions with calibrated probabilities in one pass; trained on browser-agent steps and general decision data, they pick a web agent's next operation and element.
- [OpenWebVoyager/OpenWebVoyager-IL](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-725f9a8225f0f9c40d93/) — Date not recorded
  An Idefics2-based multimodal web-agent checkpoint trained by imitation learning on trajectories collected with a web-agent framework.
- [OS-Genesis 4B WA](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-807dadc7dc4ba995bbbe/) — Date not recorded
  The WebArena-oriented InternVL2-4B checkpoint trained on web trajectories generated through OS-Genesis reverse task synthesis, predicting the next thought and action from an instruction, history and page.
- [OS-Genesis 7B WA](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-93c98e3b5aea945e0877/) — Date not recorded
  The WebArena-oriented Qwen2-VL-7B checkpoint trained on web trajectories generated through OS-Genesis reverse task synthesis, predicting the next thought and action from an instruction, history and page.
- [OS-Genesis 8B WA](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-f2c34526651f5f57cf98/) — Date not recorded
  WebArena-oriented InternVL2-8B checkpoint trained on web trajectories from OS-Genesis reverse task synthesis; the card's example prompt pairs a screenshot and accessibility tree with the instruction and action history.
- [OTA-v1](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-404c719910f8f4a37149/) — Date not recorded
  OTA-v1 is a fine-tuned browser-agent checkpoint designed to plan browser actions, use tools and maintain context across multi-step web tasks.
- [PAE LLaVA 7B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-5d61c1b5fc8dd650518d/) — Date not recorded
  A released LLaVA checkpoint for visual browser interaction from the PAE autonomous task-proposal and policy-learning system.
- [Plan-and-Act Actor 70B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-8a284d122d7dcf23479d/) — Date not recorded
  Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct-based executor checkpoint from the Plan-and-Act web-agent framework that translates high-level plans into browser actions.
- [Plan-and-Act Planner 70B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-3932d69c6b6f9b2a7ba8/) — Date not recorded
  The planner component of Plan-and-Act, generating structured high-level plans that an executor turns into web actions.
- [Qwen-CUA](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-a886fea306211d527701/) — Date not recorded
  Qwen-CUA is a screenshot-based computer-use model that generates grounded keyboard and mouse actions. Its release includes weights and a browser reference runtime.
- [Qwen3-8B-FormFactory-GRPO-LoRA](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-4a3799bd1b6db15afffe/) — Date not recorded
  Qwen3-8B-FormFactory-GRPO-LoRA is a browser form-filling adapter for Qwen3-8B, trained with supervised initialization and online GRPO using real browser execution.
- [Qwen3-8B-FormFactory-SFT-LoRA](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-994d4cf27b0931fb53e3/) — Date not recorded
  A Qwen3-8B LoRA adapter fine-tuned on 992 demonstrations for filling web forms in FormFactory, released with instructions for loading it through PEFT.
- [Qwen3.5-4B-Browser-Agent-SFT-FP8](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-6eda678ca03ea2b10d2d/) — Date not recorded
  An FP8 Qwen3.5-4B checkpoint fine-tuned on browser-agent trajectories with structured actions. Its card provides vLLM serving settings and reports BrowseWebApp-Bench v2 results.
- [UIX-Qwen2-Mind2Web](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-592e434848a1514efef0/) — Date not recorded
  UIX-Qwen2 further fine-tuned on the Mind2Web training set; the MultiUI paper evaluates it on predicting the next web action from a screenshot, the task and previous actions.
- [WebJudge 7B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-772161f06c2b3d4978b5/) — Date not recorded
  A visual trajectory judge that retains selected intermediate screenshots to evaluate whether a web agent completed its task.
- [WebRL Llama 3.1 8B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-a6ee20fea67438d91e06/) — Date not recorded
  A WebRL browser-policy checkpoint for performing actions in the five website environments of WebArena.
- [WebRL outcome reward model 8B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-d1e85aeb6d96859c109d/) — Date not recorded
  The outcome-supervision checkpoint used by WebRL to score browser trajectories during policy training.
- [WebRL-GLM-4-9B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-f979ca583090405aadee/) — Date not recorded
  A GLM-based WebRL model released for browser tasks across five WebArena websites. Its card reports evaluation results on WebArena-Lite.
- [webrl-llama-3.1-70b](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-5371bf588e7790a66ff0/) — Date not recorded
  A released Llama-3.1-70B WebRL model for operating five WebArena websites. Its model card reports WebArena-Lite results and points to inference requirements.
- [WebShepherd 3B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-72f7fe0211c6551b945f/) — Date not recorded
  A public text-based process-reward checkpoint from the Web-Shepherd collection for scoring web-agent decisions against task checklists.
- [WebThinker-QwQ-32B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-e058cc2487a805f976fd/) — Date not recorded
  WebThinker-QwQ-32B is a released reasoning model for searching and navigating web pages while drafting research reports. Its card describes training on preferences derived from reasoning trajectories.
- [WebThinker-R1-32B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-8076f65dc82b4dcea18c/) — Date not recorded
  Released reasoning checkpoint for web research that searches and navigates pages through interactive clicks while drafting reports from gathered information.
- [WebThinker-R1-7B](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-564c980a531e7204447c/) — Date not recorded
  A released 7B reasoning model for web research that searches and navigates pages through interactive elements while drafting reports.
- [WebVIA-Agent](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-62724cba1b5814215cf7/) — Date not recorded
  GLM-4.1V-9B-based web exploration agent from WebVIA that reads a page screenshot and DOM tree and picks click, type or select actions to reach each interactive UI state for UI-to-code work.

## 工程与产业

- [1Claw Browser Bridge](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-1clawai-browser-bridge/) — Date not recorded

- [Webhands](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-agentpostmortem-webhands/) — Date not recorded
  Webhands is a browser computer-use agent that automates seller and logistics web dashboards lacking APIs via Cloudflare Browser Rendering.
- [WebInteractionTools MCP](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-tcehjaava-webinteractiontools/) — Date not recorded
  Playwright-based Model Context Protocol server providing webpage navigation, viewport screenshots, clicking, and form filling for AI agents.
- [WebLlama](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-mcgill-nlp-webllama/) — Date not recorded
  Training, evaluation and integration tooling for dialogue-guided web agents, including Llama fine-tuning workflows and BrowserGym examples built around WebLINX.
- [WebMCP](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-webmachinelearning-webmcp/) — Date not recorded
  A client-side web API proposal that lets pages expose structured JavaScript tools to browser agents. Agents can invoke page functions while sharing the visible interface and context with users.
- [WebMCP Deadbolt](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-faxanfm-webmcp-deadbolt/) — Date not recorded
  Fail-closed capability gating, structured refusal, and adversarial defense middleware for WebMCP browser commerce agents.
- [WebMCP Stack](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-souravinsights-webmcp-stack/) — Date not recorded
  Generates typed, human-reviewed WebMCP browser tools directly from existing OpenAPI and backend contracts.
- [webmcp-gen](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-nidhicodes-webmcp-gen/) — Date not recorded
  Automatically generates callable WebMCP tool schemas from live webpages without requiring site-owner instrumentation.
- [WebMCPcss](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-cochinoraptor-webmcpcss/) — Date not recorded
  Extends the WebMCP standard by declaring agent-callable website actions and semantic affordances directly through CSS rules.
- [webmcpify](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-tuejon-webmcpify/) — Date not recorded
  An agent skill that adds WebMCP tools to existing web apps. It inventories app functions, asks for approval of a tool manifest and checks tool effects in Chrome.
- [WebMirage](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-moontea0416-webmirage/) — Date not recorded
  WebMirage is an adversarial red-teaming framework that crafts visual perturbations to hijack web agents from grounding to browser execution.
- [WebOperator](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-kagnlp-weboperator/) — Date not recorded
  A web agent using action-aware tree search to explore tasks and backtrack, checking page reconstruction and accounting for actions that may change website state.
- [WebOperator](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-kazkozdev-weboperator/) — Date not recorded
  WebOperator is an AI browser agent and MCP server that automates a user's existing Chrome profile through the Chrome DevTools Protocol.
- [WebOperator Experiments](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-mahirlabibdihan-weboperator-experiments/) — Date not recorded
  Browsable viewers show WebOperator task trajectories for WebArena and WebArena-lite, with screenshots loaded from a linked Hugging Face dataset.
- [Webpage MCP](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-mcpland-webpage-mcp/) — Date not recorded
  Turns any existing web application into an in-page Model Context Protocol server so AI agents can navigate and invoke DOM actions.
- [WebQA Agent](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-migoxlab-webqa-agent/) — Date not recorded
  WebQA Agent is an autonomous multimodal web testing agent that audits webpage functionality, performance, and visual UX without test scripts.
- [WebRain](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-prokopis3-webrain/) — Date not recorded
  Single-binary Rust Model Context Protocol server for LLM-driven browser automation and web scraping across three browser engines.
- [WebRL](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-thudm-webrl/) — Date not recorded
  Training pipeline that evolves web tasks from prior attempts and uses an outcome reward model to refine browser policies.
- [WebSense MCP 2.0](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-spliffspliff70-wq-websense-mcp/) — Date not recorded
  Non-vision browser automation Model Context Protocol server exposing 7 compact DOM-sensing and action tools.
- [WebSentinel detection demo](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-wxl-lxw-websentinel/) — Date not recorded
  Demo of WebSentinel's context check: GPT-4o judges pre-extracted pop-up, email and message HTML segments from 144 injected webpage samples against the page context and reports how many it misses.
- [WebTactix](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-40795d2c86388ae89f43/) — Date not recorded
  A web agent that simplifies accessibility trees, explores candidate actions in parallel and stores visited pages in a semantic tree to guide later decisions and recovery.
- [WebThinker](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-ruc-nlpir-webthinker/) — Date not recorded
  WebThinker empowers large reasoning models to autonomously search, browse webpages, and draft research reports during deep thinking.
- [WebVLN research implementation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-webvln-webvln/) — Date not recorded
  Training and evaluation code for question-guided website navigation that combines visual observations with web-specific HTML information.
- [WellSet WebMCP](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-kinhikars-wellset-webmcp/) — Date not recorded
  Shared 96-well laboratory experiment canvas built on WebMCP where scientists and browser agents co-design protocols.
- [Windows Agent Browser (winbrowse)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-civitasmass-windows-agent-browser/) — Date not recorded
  Allows Claude Code and Codex running in Windows or WSL2 to drive the user's signed-in Chrome or Edge browser.
- [winter40611 Browser Agent](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-winter40611-browser-agent/) — Date not recorded
  Spring Boot, LangChain4j, and MCP agent backend enabling remote PC and browser automation via WeChat iLink.
- [Witness](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-ericfinland-witness/) — Date not recorded
  Local-first observability and execution trace recorder for AI browser agents.
- [wix-editor-mcp](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-naieum-wix-editor-mcp/) — Date not recorded
  Model Context Protocol server for automating page layout, text, images, and SEO inside the classic Wix Editor.
- [WMA Web Agent](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-kyle8581-wma-agents/) — Date not recorded
  A web agent uses a learned world model to predict consequential page changes before choosing an action. The repository includes trajectory processing and inference code.
- [Workday Expense & Invoice Assistant (tnehelper)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-guoyuqi2002-tnehelper/) — Date not recorded
  Desktop and browser automation agent that reconciles invoice documents against Workday travel-and-expense web portals.
- [Workday HCM Browser Automation](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-browser-automation-hub-workday-hcm-browser-automation/) — Date not recorded
  A browser automation scaffold for Workday HCM that uses Puppeteer or Anchor Browser to run HR and payroll workflows through the application interface.
- [Workflow Use](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-browser-use-workflow-use/) — Date not recorded
  Turns a recorded browser session or a Browser Use agent run into a parameterized workflow file that replays deterministically with Playwright, falling back to an LLM agent when a step fails.
- [Workflow-Guided Exploration research code](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-stanfordnlp-wge/) — Date not recorded
  Research code that learns browser-control policies by constraining exploration with workflows induced from demonstrations, with a Selenium-based MiniWoB environment and experiment harness.
- [WorkForceAgent-R1](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-night-chen-workforceagent-r1/) — Date not recorded
  Web-task reward functions, WorkArena data processing and GRPO training configurations for reasoning-oriented language agents.
- [Worldline](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-artemyx-datascientist-worldline/) — Date not recorded
  Modular, user-controlled AI browser kernel for structured web parsing, navigation, and workflow automation.
- [Xiaozhuang DSH](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-niushuanan-xiaozhuang-dsh/) — Date not recorded
  Plugin-enhanced DeepSeek Harness distribution pre-packaged with desktop computer use and browser automation.
- [Xihe Agent (羲和)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-jiugao-yi-xihe-agent/) — Date not recorded
  Single-process tool-calling agent runtime operating across CLI, enterprise chat gateways, desktop GUI, and real browsers.
- [Xiryn](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-souravberaakaspeed-xiryn/) — Date not recorded
  Self-hosted personal agent app that gives each agent its own Docker Linux desktop with Chromium, operated through screenshots and xdotool clicks and keys, with live take-over, memory, a vault and Telegram control.
- [xiyiyiru chrome-mcp (DrissionPage MCP)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-xiyiyiru-chrome-mcp/) — Date not recorded
  Model Context Protocol server for browser automation built on DrissionPage.
- [Yad (يد)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-holistis-al-yad/) — Date not recorded
  Self-hostable, privacy-first local AI browser automation agent and Chrome extension.
- [Your Browser Agent](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-rakeshnirzari1-your-browser-agent/) — Date not recorded
  Connects Claude Code, Cursor, and MCP clients to the user's real logged-in Chrome browser.
- [Yurei](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-memel06-yurei/) — Date not recorded
  Chrome extension and lightweight CLI allowing local AI agents to read and control active browser tabs.
- [Yutori](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-eff580f3185f2a36c265/) — Date not recorded
  Yutori's web-agent platform: the Navigator computer-use model API (n1.5 for browsers, n2 for full desktops), task APIs for browsing, research and Scout web monitoring, and the Delegate personal agent.

- [zen-mcp](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-sh6drack-zen-mcp/) — Date not recorded

- [Zendriver](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-cdpdriver-zendriver/) — Date not recorded
  A Python browser automation library built on Chrome DevTools Protocol, offering page navigation, element lookup, screenshots and browser profile management.

  Official Model Context Protocol server for controlling ZennoBrowser anti-detect browser profiles, proxies, cookies, and web actions.

- [Zoom Search](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-goofrey-zoom-search/) — Date not recorded

- [zseven-w DSH Browser](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-zseven-w-dsh-browser/) — Date not recorded
  Headless-first browser automation plugin for DeepSeek Harness.
- [zuhair-01 chrome-agent-bridge](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-zuhair-01-chrome-agent-bridge/) — Date not recorded

## 学习资源

- [Agentic UI Testing Codelab](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-a-bhimava-agentic-ui-testing/) — Date not recorded
  Hands-on developer codelab for building automated UI test suites using Antigravity CLI and Browser Use.
- [Agentic Web](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-saferl-lab-agentic-web/) — Date not recorded
  A research reading list on agents and the web, organized around development, retrieval, planning, safety and benchmarks.
- [Amazon Nova Act Samples](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-amazon-agi-labs-nova-act-samples/) — Date not recorded
  Code examples and reference applications show how to build browser workflows with Amazon Nova Act and deploy the SDK on AWS compute services.
- [Building AI Browser Agents](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-aa693ca3e8948623f31e/) — Date not recorded
  Browser-agent course with worked lessons on structured web extraction and multi-step browser interaction, followed by AgentQ search and self-correction concepts.
- [chromedp examples](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-chromedp-examples/) — Date not recorded
  A collection of 46 Go examples showing how to control Chrome with chromedp, including page interaction, screenshots, tabs, and protocol operations.
- [Computer-Use Agents: An Overview of Terminal-Based and GUI-Based Approaches](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-cdxeve-awesome-computer-use-agents/) — Date not recorded
  A curated reading list and accompanying report organize computer-use research, including agents that act through web, desktop, and mobile interfaces.
- [Google Developer Codelab: Automated UI Testing with Antigravity (Agy) CLI, BrowserMCP, Playwright, and the Browser Agent](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-derailed-dash-agentic-ui-testing/) — Date not recorded
  A hands-on codelab for creating browser-based UI tests with Antigravity CLI, BrowserMCP and a Playwright agent skill, using an included demo application.
- [H.AI cookbook](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-hcompai-hai-cookbook/) — Date not recorded
  The H.AI cookbook provides notebooks and guides for deploying Holo models and invoking their web navigation and interface localization capabilities.
- [linux-use-recipes](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-javimosch-linux-use-recipes/) — Date not recorded
  A collection of recipes for automating Linux desktop applications and websites with linux-use. It covers browser setup, accessibility pitfalls, posting workflows and verification.
- [Midscene Examples](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-web-infra-dev-midscene-example/) — Date not recorded
  Midscene Examples provides runnable AI automation and testing cases for web, Android, iOS and desktop interfaces, including YAML workflows and SDK integrations.
- [Open Operator](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-openhands-open-operator/) — Date not recorded
  Open Operator collects resources on computer-use agents, with task categories, benchmark summaries, and pages describing existing agent systems.
- [Plasmate Notebooks](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-plasmate-labs-notebooks/) — Date not recorded
  Plasmate Notebooks teaches use of an agent-oriented browser engine through Jupyter examples covering page structure, token comparisons, batch extraction, and research workflows.
- [Steel Cookbook](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-steel-dev-steel-cookbook/) — Date not recorded
  A collection of Steel browser automation examples covering browser libraries, screenshot-driven computer-use models and agent frameworks in several programming languages.
- [The TinyFish Cookbook](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/github-tinyfish-io-tinyfish-cookbook/) — Date not recorded
  A collection of sample applications and recipes for TinyFish web agents, covering page retrieval, structured search, and multi-step browser automation.
- [Web Browser Automation with Agents (smolagents tutorial)](https://awesomecua.com/zh/resources/url-5857c840372512cfe3fc/) — Date not recorded
  Versioned tutorial building a vision-guided CodeAgent with Selenium/Helium browser tools, screenshot callbacks and example web-navigation tasks.
